# PROJECT BRAIN — ServiceMgmt

> Internal business OS for a multi-channel computer & CCTV shop — retail (in-store + online), wholesale, and a repair service centre on one shared inventory and accounting backbone.

**Stack:** Next.js 16.2.3 (App Router, Turbopack), React 19, PostgreSQL (Supabase), Prisma 7.7.0 (split schema), NextAuth v5 beta (JWT), Tailwind v4, shadcn/ui, Zustand 5, Sonner, Axios, React Hook Form, Zod, Razorpay, Cloudinary, Sentry.
**Repo:** `D:\AbeerSuite\service-mgmt-experiment` — branch `dev`.
**Started:** 2026-04-17.
**Last updated:** 2026-09-30.

This file is the **only doc that carries status**. Architecture lives in [ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md). Setup/runbook lives in [README.md](README.md). Contributor rules live in [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md). POS keyboard conventions live in [docs/pos-conventions.md](docs/pos-conventions.md). For the authoritative history, read `git log`.

---

## 🎯 Goal

Replace paper/spreadsheets across the whole business with one app:

- **Repair tickets** — Received → Diagnosing → Repairing → … → Returned, with role handoffs (Front Desk, Technician, Despatch, Admin).
- **Accounting** — Tally-style GL: vouchers, GST/HSN, BS / P&L / Day Book / Trial Balance, audit trails.
- **Unified inventory** — one `InvProduct` master feeds storefront, repair, POS, wholesale.
- **POS** — counter-based tills, shift lifecycle, sale returns.
- **CRM & leads** — kanban pipeline reusing the Ticket engine.
- **Customer shop + portal** — `/shop` + `/portal` (separate `CustomerPortalCredential` auth).
- **Org / RBAC** — multi-branch, multi-department, rank-gated roles with `area:operation:scope` permissions.

Checked in the browser on the local copy: TEST B3 Ledger (Indirect Expenses, ₹100 Dr, keyword b3test) made from Paid-from, Ctrl+Enter → the edit box showed all four.
- **2026-10-01 — POS ⇄ convert from the mode dropdown.** An existing POS Estimate / Order opened at the till now offers **⇄ Sale** (and **⇄ Order** from an estimate; nothing converts back to Estimate) in the Voucher-type dropdown instead of plain links. Picking one keeps the cart (`convertFrom` in `posDraftStore`), shows a "Converting from <ref> ×" chip, and the save links back via `refVoucherId` so the source shows Converted; × reopens the source. Before, switching mode from an open estimate started an unrelated bill. One guard for every convert path — `src/modules/pos/lib/convertSource.ts` — used by `/api/pos/sale`, `/api/pos/order` (new `refVoucherId`, estimate sources only) and the Day Book convert API; a second convert of the same source gets 409. `/api/orders/inv/[id]` returns `convertedTo` so a converted source shows its ⇄ options disabled. Browser-checked up to the convert screen and ×; no sale was saved (dev server writes to live).
- **2026-10-02 — Unsurfaced old pages removed.** A reference scan of all 276 pages (who mentions each address outside its own folder, plus redirect-only pages), each hit read by hand, local copy checked for stored links (chat messages: none). **Deleted:** `/office/inventory/configuration` (scaffold), `/office/accountancy/dashboard` and `/office/customers/dashboard` ("coming soon" stubs), `/office/accountancy/vouchers/[id]` and `/office/accountancy/journal-entries/[id]` (old voucher addresses that only forwarded), `/receipt/payment/[id]` (old Receipt/Payment print address, replaced 29 Sep; every till runs v1.10.x). **Kept on purpose:** `/office/hr/me` carries the My Info tick (`ui:sidebar:hr:me`) — removing it is a permissions change; the section landings (`/office/accountancy`, `/office/customers`, `/office/hr`, …) are what the sidebar uses; `/receipt/invoice/[id]` is the live bill print, only its name is old. **Portal:** the header and home "My tickets" now go straight to `/portal/repairs` ("My Repairs"); `/portal/tickets` and `/portal/settings` stay as forwarders for customers' bookmarks; the e2e test covers both. **Optional Vouchers** (report + its `/api/inventory/vouchers/optional`) deleted too (owner): Day Book's Optional / Inventory ticks list the same documents. **Orders & Estimates** (`/office/accountancy/orders-estimates`) deleted with its `ConvertVoucherButton` (owner): Day Book already lists every estimate and order (its "Optional" tick shows only documents) and each row's ⋮ menu carries "Convert to …" from the same `CONVERT_MAP` and convert API.
- **2026-10-02 — Batch 3b: database clean-up (migration `20261002090000_db_cleanup_b3b`, not yet deployed).** **RELEASE GATE: every till must show the new build on Admin → Counters before the `d_office` push** — a replica keeps every column it was ever given and ships whole rows, and only this build's push keeps the columns the office has (`SyncRegistryEntry.columns`, `bd3fd78c`); an older till's voucher uploads would wait in its queue until it updates (nothing lost). - **2026-10-01 — "Unsaved changes" asks only when the voucher really differs.** Batch 2, item 4. Payment / Receipt / Contra / notes (GL), Journal, Sale / Purchase (Trading), Physical Stock, Manufacturing Journal and Branch Transfer flipped an `isDirtyRef` on any `change` / `input` event bubbling out of the form. Wrong both ways: a pick from a ledger, party or product list, a date box or a note count fires no such event, so Esc or Back left without asking and the work was lost; typing a letter and deleting it again left the flag set, so it asked about nothing. Trading also never cleared it after save-and-continue, so the next blank bill asked too. New [`useFormChanges`](src/shared/hooks/useFormChanges.ts): each form passes a snapshot of what the user enters (display values — live stock, computed tax, the party's balance — left out), and it is "changed" while that differs from its starting point. The starting point is taken at the user's first press inside the form (pointer or key, capture phase, portals included), so everything the screen fills in by itself before then — an edit prefill, a duplicate, the last-used cash ledger, a settle-a-bill preload — is where the user started, not a change. A save that resets the form calls `markClean()`. Trading's browser "leave site?" prompt follows the same answer. The GL form's note counts are compared as counted notes (a box typed "0" or emptied is no count).
- **2026-10-01 — Paged lists read once per change and always match their boxes.** Batch 2, item 2. Five screens kept `page` in their own state and fetched by hand. A filter change read TWICE — the new filters on the old page, then again after an effect reset the page to 1, the slower reply winning — and nothing told a late reply from the current one. New [`usePagedQuery`](src/shared/hooks/usePagedQuery.ts): the page is stored WITH the filters it was turned under and reads as 1 the moment they differ, so one change is one render, one URL, one read; the read is `useApiResource` (late replies dropped, rows kept on screen through a realtime refresh). Converted: Contacts (the reset effect and the hand debounce gone), a group's Ledgers page, Pending Vouchers & Stock (dates still apply on Refresh; search / type / branch / page follow their boxes; the Stock tab is read once opened and then follows the filters too — it used to stay on the old branch until reloaded; only the visible tab follows the books live), and the POS Sales Register list (search now debounced 300 ms — it read on every keystroke; the counter-filter reset effect gone). Account Ledger's statement is not paged but had the same race — pick ledger A then B, or change the dates twice, and the older answer could land last; it is `useApiResource` keyed on ledger + dates now. Two things changed on purpose: Refresh keeps the list on screen while it re-reads (it blanked behind a spinner), and the group Ledgers page no longer blanks on every voucher posted anywhere. Proved in real Chromium against a fake server: from page 3, a filter change makes exactly one request, at page 1; a slow reply for the old search never lands over the new one.
- **2026-10-01 — Dropdown lists close only when you really leave.** Batch 2, item 7. [`useComboboxNav`](src/shared/hooks/useComboboxNav.ts) closed the list 150 ms after ANY blur of the box — a timer that existed so a row's mousedown could fire first. Dragging the list's scrollbar blurred the box and shut the list mid-drag; clicking out and straight back in got the list shut under the mouse by the first blur's timer. Now the dropdown's outermost element takes `cb.dropdownMouseDown` (preventDefault + stopPropagation), so a press anywhere inside it — row, "+ create", scrollbar — never takes focus off the input; a blur therefore always means the user left, and the list closes at once (focus moving to the box's own clear button excepted). `blurDelayMs` is gone. The ledger, group and product pickers use it. Proved in real Chromium with visible scrollbars: scrollbar drag keeps the list open and focus in the box, outside click closes at once, quick click-back keeps it open, a row still picks — and the old hook fails the click-back case.
- **2026-10-01 — Something made from a picker shows in every row, not only its own.** Batch 2, item 6. A tab never hears its own write over realtime (the broadcast does not echo to the sender), so a product or ledger created with "+ create" in one row reached only that row's private overlay; the rest of the page waited for somebody else's save. **Products:** the picker now calls [`invalidateProductCatalog`](src/shared/hooks/useProductCatalog.ts) — waits out a sync already on the wire (it may have asked before the product existed), then a delta brings the server's full row to every picker. Deliberately not an in-memory upsert of the quick-create row: the till reads the same catalogue and that row carries no tax rate. **Ledgers:** [`invalidateGlAccounts`](src/shared/hooks/useGlAccounts.ts) does the same for the shared chart (no-op when no screen holds it), and `GlAccountCombobox` now calls it AND the caller's `onMastersChanged` — a prop that existed for screens with their own ledger list but was wired nowhere. Wired on Trading (charges + tenders; its ledger and services reads also drop a superseded answer now), Manufacturing Journal (its list moved onto `useApiResource` with the "ledger" topic), Settings → POS Services, Admin → Counters, Payment Methods and Opening Balance. The per-row overlays stay: they bridge the round trip and keep a ledger reachable in a filtered slice. Noticed, not changed: Ctrl+Enter on such an overlay-only ledger hands the edit dialog `{id, name, groupId}` only, so a save there would blank the particulars entered at create.
- **2026-10-01 — Bank reconciliation matches the same day and the same amount, strictly.** Owner: *"we must entry on same day same amount strictly. if yet some UTR, UPI/IMPS ref, cheque number matching we can hint them so book date can be modified"*. `planAutoMatch` ([bankReconciliation.ts](src/modules/accountancy/lib/bankReconciliation.ts)) pairs only same-day, same-amount entries (a shared reference still picks between two same-day candidates; a tie is left alone); the cross-day path, the 7-day window, its toolbar box and the API's `windowDays` are gone. `createMatch` refuses rows from different days and names the book entry whose date to set. Book-it on several lines takes one day's lines only, and a voucher saved on another date is not matched — the toast says which date to set. The old amount-only hint ("possible: UV-3519 · 28 days away", which sent 13 Aug's ₹100 UPI to an unrelated swipe entry) is replaced by a **reference hint** — same amount, same UTR / UPI / IMPS ref / net-banking id / cheque no, different day — that links to the voucher to correct its date and ticks nothing. [`extractReferenceTokens`](src/shared/lib/import/bankStatement.ts) no longer counts a counterparty account number ("TRANSFER FROM 4698313162099" — the same on every transfer from that sender) as a reference; `referenceTokensOf` drops one even when an import stored it as the line's reference. On SBI CC A/c 873 all 107 ticks are same-day (none affected) and no reference hint shows today — the book entries carry no bank reference.

- **2026-10-01 — The reconciliation lock follows every book change, on every ledger, by the owner's three rules.** Owner: *"bank never do backdated entry. lock move is book have any backdated entry"*, *"not only bank, every ledger"*, then *"merge reset lock fully, opening balance change reset lock, editing voucher move the lock only if amount changed for the ledger"*. All in [`reconciliation.ts`](src/modules/accountancy/lib/reconciliation.ts). **Voucher edit** — the GL voucher edit, the Journal Entries edit (it had no retreat at all) and the Sale / Purchase / POS edit ([`reapplyTradingVoucherInPlace`](src/modules/accountancy/lib/reapplyTradingVoucher.ts), which had none either; it now takes a required `previousDate`) — retreats only `changedLedgers`: a ledger whose set of amounts in the voucher changed, or every ledger in it when the date moved (the amount leaves one day for another), from the earlier of the two dates. **Ticks survive edits**: all three paths delete and re-mint the voucher's GL lines, which left every bank tick stale even on a narration-only edit — and on a statement line before the lock, stuck for good. `carryStatementMatches` re-points each ticked old line at its new twin (same ledger, side, amount); one without a twin stays stale, and its ledger is exactly one whose lock just moved. **Opening balance** — `resetReconciliationOnOpening` clears the lock outright when the signed opening really changes, called before the write in all four ledger saves (`accounts/[id]/opening`, `gl-accounts/[id]`, `ledgers/[id]`, `accounts/[id]`) and for the suspense ledger in `reconcileOpeningSuspense`; [`saveOpeningBills`](src/modules/accountancy/lib/openingBills.ts) clears the party's and the suspense's only when the opening TOTAL changes (re-splitting bills or converting the scalar is not a change). `gl-accounts/[id]` also gains the `reconcileOpeningSuspense()` every other opening write already made. **Merge** ([`convertParty.ts`](src/modules/customer/lib/convertParty.ts)) clears the winner's lock. Cancel, park, restore and delete keep the date retreat — a voucher appearing or vanishing changes every ledger in it. Type-checked and the change rule run on sample edits; not run against a database (the dev server writes to live).

- **2026-10-01 — Bank Reconciliation shows where books and bank stop agreeing.** Owner: *"need to know from where the book and bank mismatch started"*, then *"the balance should have continuous colour till condition match"* — and *"same date and amount does not mean same transaction, see 13 aug"*. The Balance column of [`BankReconciliationPage`](src/modules/accountancy/components/BankReconciliationPage.tsx) is now an orange band (emerald at first — it blended into the green of ticked rows), unbroken (Books rows carry it too, with no figure), from the first day of the period through the last day that is **proven**: closes with the bank's printed balance equal to the books' AND has every row on both sides ticked. A matching total is not proof — two bank lines summing to one book entry may be two different sums of money — so an unticked day ends the band even when the balances agree. The rule is [`agreementRun`](src/modules/accountancy/lib/bankReconAgreement.ts), pure and in its own file so a script can run it on real data; `loadReconciliation` returns `summary.agreedThrough` + `summary.breakAt` (date, gap, unticked count) from the reads it already made — no new query. The legend names the last agreed day and the break (hover says why); the Split view carries the same band on the statement pane. Checked against SBI CC A/c 873 as it stands: band ends 12 Aug (13 Aug's ₹13,255 + ₹100 vs CONTRA 2442 ₹13,355 is unticked); with those three ticked it runs to 16 Aug and breaks on 17 Aug at ₹2,750 (a GOASSPARSH NHM NEFT not in books). Auto-match is unchanged — whether same-day same-amount should only suggest is the owner's open call. **Bank lines before the book-start year are inside the opening balance.** The upload keeps lines dated ON the lock day open, so SBI's six 31 Mar lines (net +₹15,060) were saved — already inside the ₹5,11,906.06 Cr opening, with no voucher to tick them against: the Summary showed a false ₹15,060 difference and the lock could never pass 31 Mar. Owner: *"we need to consider the first available date to match the FY"* — *"the book start year has to take care and next years are going in continuation"*. [`openingCover`](src/modules/accountancy/lib/bankReconAgreement.ts) takes the book-start year (the day after `openingBalanceDate` when set — every ledger save clears it, so in practice — the year of the ledger's first book entry) and reads the bank's opening off the first statement line available in it (its balance before its own amount). Equal to the books' opening to the paisa → every line dated before that year is `inOpening`: greyed with an "in opening balance" tag, no tick or Book-it, left out of "not in books", the colour band and the lock check (`assertLockAllowed`), refused by auto-match and Match. Not equal → nothing excused, and the Summary names both openings. Later years need nothing — they run on from the day before. `loadOpeningCover` is four small reads (first book entry, sums before the year, two statement lines). SBI CC A/c 873: openings agree at ₹5,11,906.06, six lines covered, Summary difference ₹0. Still open: the "possible: … 28 days away" hint can point at the wrong entry when the real partner is a split.

- **2026-10-01 — Contacts never show one party's details under another's name.** Batch 2, item 5. Contacts → All / Person / Companies read the open party's profile and statement by hand; only the All page cancelled a stale profile read, and none cancelled the statement. Click party A, then B, and A's slower answer could land last — A's balance and bills shown under B's heading. New [`usePartyDetail` / `usePartyTransactions`](src/modules/customer/hooks/usePartyProfile.ts) on `useApiResource` tie every reply to the party (and period) it was asked for. The statement keeps its rules: read while its tab is on screen, only marked stale by the books moving while it is not, re-read quietly on the way back in (AGENTS.md §7); a toast only when there is nothing to show. The tab a newly opened party starts on is derived (a choice belongs to the party it was made for) instead of being set when the profile arrives, and a profile save still patches the list row's name — from the payload `reload()` now returns. The three pages lose their `txStale` / `txLoading` / detail state, the auto-load effect and the suppressed lint rules.
- **2026-10-01 — Reports never show an older answer over the one you asked for.** Batch 2, item 4. Sixteen screens loaded by hand — `useCallback(load)` + `useEffect(load)` with the lint rule switched off + a separate `useRealtimeTopics` — and none could tell a late reply from the current one: change the dates twice quickly and the slower answer for the first dates could land last, under the second dates. Trial Balance, P&L, Balance Sheet, Cash Flow, Fund Flow, Statistics, Ratio Analysis, GST Liability, HSN Summary, the GST summary view, Usable Fund, Outstandings, Group Report, the Book pages and the customer Loyalty panel now read through [`useApiResource`](src/shared/hooks/useApiResource.ts), which drops a reply for a URL the screen has left. The hook gained three options, all backward-compatible for its 70 callers: **`topics`** (re-read on the realtime signal as a refresh, figures kept on screen), **`keepPrevious`** (a URL change keeps the old figures up until the new ones land — Group Report's deliberate "a date change must not blank the report"), and **`reload()` returns the payload** it brought back. The twenty BI pages share [`useBiQuery`](src/modules/bi/hooks/useBiQuery.tsx), which now lets only its newest request write. Two things changed on purpose: a manual **Refresh keeps the report on screen** (it used to blank it behind a spinner), and a group you collapse on Trial Balance / P&L / Balance Sheet **stays collapsed** across refreshes (the default is still all-open). The Book page's jump to a book's single ledger fires once per period instead of on every live update.
- **2026-10-01 — Money boxes take only a valid amount.** Batch 2, item 3. Cash Drop, POS Expenditure, Shift Open's vault top-up, Settings → Services' price and Org Defaults' clearance cutoff each cleaned typing with a hand-written `replace(/[^\d.]/g, "")`, which keeps every dot — "1.2.3" could be typed and saved as 1.2 (or read as nothing). They are the shared [`NumericInput`](src/shared/components/NumericInput.tsx) now (`mode="decimal" min={0}`): one dot, cleaned on each keystroke, clamped when the box is left. The Deal form's value box did no cleaning at all and silently saved a deal with no value for "1.2.3"; it is `NumericInput` too, read through the shared `parseRupeesToPaise`. Three exact private copies of the shared rule (Credit Limit settings, the POS price cell, GST rates) call `parseDecimalInput` / `parseDigitsInput` instead. The payment-link box keeps its own two-decimal rule on purpose.
- **2026-10-01 — A POS line note comes back whole when a sale is edited, even if the product name has " · " in it.** Batch 2, item 2. The POS stores a goods line as `"<sku> — <name> · <note>"`. Printing already read it back through [`splitLineDescription`](src/shared/lib/print/lineDescription.ts), which matches the exact SKU + name prefix, but the POS sale edit / convert and the POS order / estimate edit split on the first `" · "` by hand, and so did SL Search — a product called "Cable · 2m" gave the note "2m · red" and put "2m" into an empty note. They all use `splitLineDescription` now, and the three places that write the text go through the new `composeLineDescription` beside it, so the writer and the reader are one pair. A note typed on an office-made sale also survives a POS edit (the hand split dropped a note with no `" · "`). The permanent fix — the note as its own column — needs a migration and is left for Batch 3.
- **2026-10-01 — POS offers re-check when shipping, a charge or the round-off changes.** Batch 2 of the "find patches" sweep, item 1. **Bug:** [`usePosBilling`](src/modules/pos/hooks/usePosBilling.ts) re-validated coupons and free-grant offers on a hand-picked signature (cart + tier + loyalty), but the server judges the free grant's minimum bill on `billAmountIncl`, which also moves with shipping, manual charges and the round-off. Change one of those and the screen kept showing an offer the checkout would refuse (or hid one it would give). **Fix:** the bill the coupon routes are sent (`couponBill` — account, new-customer flag, tier ₹, loyalty ₹, `billAmountIncl`, goods lines) is built once, and the re-validation signature **is** that payload, so nothing the server reads can be left out again. `runValidate` and the offers panel both send it. No server change.
- **2026-09-30 — The super admin can sign in on the desktop app.** Owner, on Counter 94: the env super admin got *"Invalid email or password"* on the till, because the `office` build shipped `SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL` alone and `checkSuperAdminEnv` needs the password too. `COUNTER_ENV_SUPER_ADMIN_KEYS` in [`counterEnv.ts`](scripts/counterEnv.ts) now carries `SUPER_ADMIN_PASSWORD` as well — `office` only, so `counter` builds still go without. The cost, accepted by the owner: a full-authority login in plaintext in `resources/counter.env` on every till and in every installer. The owner will set a strong password before the next release. Remove the key before any public desktop release.

- **2026-09-30 — The line note (SL no / colour) prints on every bill, behind a new template switch.** Owner, from Counter 94 with a serial typed in a line's Note box: *"is the sl no printing ?"* — it was not on the counter slip. **Cause:** POS stores the note inside the line text (`"<sku> — <name> · <note>"`), and each print read that text its own way: a new sale's silent slip (`print/pos` from `PosScreen`) sent only the product name; the till's reprint / **Update & Print** (`print/voucher`) had the note in the payload but [`thermal.js`](hardware-bridge/thermal.js) never printed it; the invoice page printed it glued to the name; the office Day Book print put the whole `"<sku> — <name> · <note>"` under the item. **Fix:** one reader, [`splitLineDescription`](src/shared/lib/print/lineDescription.ts) → `{ name, note }` (POS text, the office form's bare note, a product renamed after the sale, a name that itself contains " · "), used by [`InvoiceReceipt`](src/shared/components/print/InvoiceReceipt.tsx) and [`voucherReceiptSample`](src/modules/accountancy/lib/voucherReceiptSample.ts); the fresh-sale slip now sends `note`; the bridge prints it indented under the item on both commands; the A4 tax invoice, the A4 POS ladder table and the 80mm browser slip print it under the item too. New template flags **`showLineNote` / `showLineNoteThermal`** (Settings → Print Templates → *Line note (serial no. / colour)*), **on by default for every kind** (owner's call), hidden on Receipt / Payment and the bespoke documents. The bridge reads a missing flag as on, so a counter on this build prints the note even while the server is still on the old one. Migration `20260930120000_print_line_note` (two `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`) — **counters first, then the `d_office` deploy**. Bridge tests: `pnpm test:bridge` (18/18).

- **2026-09-30 — Product preview opens inside the app, draft or live, on its own clean page.** Owner, from a counter: *"the preview is 404 in app"*, then *"published unpublished both open in app tab, + published has open in shop/chrome option"* and *"preview should open without sidebar … and in new tab"*. **Cause:** the live-preview card (Details / photo / name) and the Products list linked to `/shop/product/<slug>?preview=1`, but the desktop app is the `APP_MODE=office` build, which leaves every `(external)` shop page out — so the link was a 404 there. Sending it to the office website instead was ruled out: a draft needs a staff session, and staff without **Can login outside counter** cannot sign in from an outside browser. **Fix:** new bare staff page [`/preview/product/[id]`](<src/app/preview/product/[id]/page.tsx>) — outside `/office`, so no sidebar or finance bar, opened in a new tab (a new app window in Electron) on the app's own sign-in. Locked on the **Products** tick through a new `LOCKED_AS` alias in [`pageAccess.ts`](src/shared/lib/auth/pageAccess.ts); signed-out → staff sign-in ([`proxy.ts`](src/proxy.ts)); stripped from the public build (`OFFICE_ONLY_APP_PATHS`) and reserved in [`storefrontSlugPath.ts`](src/shared/lib/shop/storefrontSlugPath.ts). It and the shop's product page share one loader, [`loadShopProduct`](src/shared/lib/shop/loadShopProduct.ts) (`includeHidden` = preview; returns `liveInShop`), and one body, [`ProductDetailView`](src/modules/external/shop/ProductDetailView.tsx), so the price and stock state cannot differ; in preview the purchase panel drops Share, View cart and the portal line (they would 404 in the app). A draft shows "not published to customers yet"; a live product shows **Open in shop** → [`productShopUrl`](src/shared/lib/shop/productPublic.ts) on `shop.networkservice.in`, which the app hands to Chrome — also on the product form's Live preview header and the Products list's expanded row. The shop page's `?preview=1` staff mode is **removed** (no `auth()` on the public page any more). Also 404 in the app, now pointed out: Customize Home's **View storefront** → the shop website; Ads' **Preview on shop** → the office website (the empty ad slots draw only for staff signed in there; anyone else sees live ads). **Verified on the dev server (in-app pane, local `abeersuite_dev`):** draft #1477 and live #226 previews — no sidebar, right strip, price, photos, no Share/View cart/portal, Open in shop only on the live one with the right address; form and list links point at `/preview/product/<id>` with `target=_blank`; signed-out → `307 /staff/login?callbackUrl=…`; `pageLinkIds('/preview/product/1477')` = the Products tick, no ticks → refused; `tsc` + ESLint clean. **Not yet:** a click in the packaged office app (next build), and the shop website is not up to date (its product page currently errors — owner aware).
- **2026-09-30 — The cash drawer opens by itself when a bill that moved cash prints.** Owner: *"open cash drawer on print … must open if money paper bill involved"*. **Rule (owner's pick):** a fresh POS Sale, Rcv or order advance opens it when paper money changed hands ([`movesCash`](src/modules/pos/components/TenderEntry.tsx) — cash tendered or change given), every Exp opens it (always paid from the drawer), a Sale Return opens it only on a cash refund; UPI- or card-only bills, sale/order edits and every reprint (Sales Register, Day Book, receipt pages) never do. **How:** the caller sets `openDrawer` on the bridge payload (`print/pos` directly; Rcv/Exp/Return/advance through [`printVoucherThermal`](src/shared/lib/print/silentThermalPrint.ts)'s new `opts`), and [`thermal.js`](hardware-bridge/thermal.js) `withDrawerKick` prepends the kick only when this PC's bridge config also has `cashDrawer` — a per-PC tick on [Printer Settings](src/app/(protected)/office/settings/printer-settings/page.tsx), default off, beside an **Open Drawer** test button (`drawer:open`, ignores the tick so wiring can be checked first). Kick goes FIRST so the drawer opens while the slip prints, and still opens when the paper is out. Written as full five-byte `ESC p m 25 250` on pins 2 and 5 — node-thermal-printer's `openCashDrawer()` sends only `ESC p m`, so the printer eats the next two bytes as timings. Works on all three connection types (all send raw ESC/POS); the browser-print fallback cannot kick — that counter needs the Windows driver's own drawer option. **Verified:** `pnpm test:bridge` 16/16 (two new); the real bridge forked against a fake LAN printer — tick off → no kick, tick on + cash → kick first, no flag → no kick, `drawer:open` → exactly the 10 kick bytes; card renders on the page (browser, bridge-less, so disabled). **Not yet:** a real drawer on a counter — install the build, tick the box, press Open Drawer.
- **2026-09-30 — Call follow-up popup: a missed call stays open until someone calls back, and every counter sees the calls nobody owns.** Owner: *"mention the ext no received the call or its miss call, because now days popup can be in other counter too"*, then *"miss call are not closing until the caller come and has ticket or sale … or next call (real call) has proper handling, otherwise the user will forget to call-back"*. **Miscall = under 2 s of talk** now (`MISSED_CALL_UNDER_SEC` in [`shared/lib/calls`](src/shared/lib/calls/index.ts); was exactly 0), one rule for the popup, the Call Log's red rows and filters, the customer timeline and Reconnect's miscall filter. **Popup** ([CallFollowUpPopup.tsx](src/modules/pos/components/CallFollowUpPopup.tsx)): the line under the title ends with the extension that took or dialled the call (`· Ext 21`, the trunk when the PBX sent none); a caller chip whose newest call is a miss is red; and a missed call is **locked** — no note, ticket, deal or Save, just "Missed call — call back to the caller", and no open-ticket read. It closes by itself when (1) someone really talks to the caller — that call joins the same chip as its newest call, unlocks it, and the one save covers the miss through `alsoIds`; or (2) the customer turns up — a POSTED sale/POS bill or any ticket (help, deal, repair) for them created after the miss ([`customerSeenSince`](src/shared/lib/calls/followUp.ts), asked only when an open miss has a known customer). Emergency close: a note on the CRM Call Log. The 3-day `CALL_POPUP_MAX_AGE_MS` still applies. **Which counter sees what** (`GET /api/calls?forCounter=1`, [`counterPopupScope`](src/shared/lib/calls/followUp.ts)): own extension(s) at once; a call that is **nobody's** — no extension (an unanswered incoming call records none, so these never reached any counter before) or an extension no active counter is set to (a spare Ext 45) — everywhere at once; any other counter's call everywhere after **`CALL_POPUP_PUBLIC_AFTER_MS`** (30 min). A PC with no counter bound gets only those two — which also closes the 2026-09-13 open item (office PC's icon never emptying, since it used to show every till's backlog). The counter-extension list is `unstable_cache`d (tag `counter-extensions`, dropped by a counter PATCH/DELETE; a till's own cache catches up within 10 min). No schema change. Checks: `tsc --noEmit` and ESLint clean; **not browser-verified** (the pane needs a staff sign-in) — owner to check the popup and Call Log on the live site.

- **2026-09-30 — Esc: the innermost open thing takes the key, and nothing else reacts to the same press.** Batch 1, item 5 of the "find patches" sweep. **Bug:** the six screens on the bare [`useEscape`](src/shared/hooks/useEscape.ts) (Products, Stock Summary, Reorder, Inventory Details, Group Report, Account Ledger) went back a page on the same press that closed a dialog, menu or popover. Thirteen other screens each carried a copy of `if (document.querySelector('[data-state="open"][role="dialog"]')) return` — which **cannot work under React 19**: the state update from Radix's own Esc commits in a microtask, before the window listener runs, so the dialog this press had just closed already read "closed" (the Trading voucher's `dialogOpenRef` mirror has the same race — Esc on its "Unsaved changes" prompt went back twice); it also ignored menus and selects. **Mechanism (checked in node_modules):** Radix listens on `document` capture and calls `preventDefault()` whenever it closes a layer. So the mark is the event's own `defaultPrevented`: new [`escapeLayer.ts`](src/shared/lib/ui/escapeLayer.ts) + a rewritten `useEscape(handler, { enabled, inInputs })` that skips a marked press and marks the one it acts on (one press, one action), and `useEscapeLayer(open, onClose)` for hand-made popovers (window capture — before Radix and the screen). A form hosted in a dialog owns that dialog's Esc through a slot [`DialogContent`](src/components/ui/dialog.tsx) provides, which is how Quick Actions' [`GlVoucherDialog`](src/shared/components/GlVoucherDialog.tsx) loses its blanket `preventDefault`. **Converted:** the GL / Journal / Trading / Physical Stock / Manufacturing Journal / Branch Transfer forms (one `useEscape` line each; `rootRef`, `dialogOpenRef` and the guards deleted; Trading keeps `inInputs: true`), the three customer pages, Pending / Optional Vouchers, Day Book (`useEscapeBack`), Outstandings' focus; popovers on the products page (3), [`SearchableSelect`](src/shared/components/SearchableSelect.tsx), [`useComboboxNav`](src/shared/hooks/useComboboxNav.ts) (its Esc ran after Radix, so Esc in a list inside a dialog closed the dialog), the link and Org Regions context menus; [`PartySearchAndCreate`](src/shared/components/PartySearchAndCreate.tsx) marks the press only when it clears a non-empty search. **Behaviour change:** the six bare-`useEscape` screens no longer act while typing in a text field (the products page's inline editors used to go back on Esc). **Known gap, left:** an editor inside a dialog that handles Esc in React's `onKeyDown` still runs after Radix, so the dialog closes too. **Verified in a real browser** (in-app pane, 2026-09-30) on a temporary page built from the real `Dialog` / `Select` / `DropdownMenu` / `SearchableSelect` / `useComboboxNav` / `useEscape` code, deleted afterwards: nothing open → one page action; typing in a field → none; an open Select, menu, searchable list or combobox list → only it closes, the next Esc reaches the page; a dialog → closes without the page acting; a list or combobox inside a dialog → only the list closes, the dialog stays; a hosted form (the `GlVoucherDialog` case) → clean closes through the form, typing does nothing, dirty asks, Esc on its prompt closes only the prompt; the Trading case → Esc in a field asks, Esc on the prompt goes back **exactly once**. **Then on the live site after the `d_office` deploy (Chrome, signed in, nothing saved):** Products — Esc on the Columns popover and on the New product dialog closes only them (`defaultPrevented` true at the page), plain Esc goes back; Optional Vouchers' type Select — Esc closes only it; new Sale — Esc raises "Unsaved changes", Esc on it goes back exactly one step and no voucher request is sent. One false alarm on the way: with the Chrome tab occluded (`visibilityState: hidden`) the dialog's effects had not run, so its Radix Esc listener did not exist yet and the page took the key — visible, it passes. Customers' link panel not driven (a link is a live write).
- **2026-09-30 — A power cut during a save can no longer make a counter throw away its unsent sales.** Batch 1, item 2 of the "find patches" sweep. **Cause:** [`electron/main.ts`](electron/main.ts) wrote `counter-setup.json` with a plain `writeFileSync` and read a file it could not parse as *no file* — and "no file" means "this PC holds no counter", so boot emptied the replica, `_outbox` included. A damaged `counter-installation-id.json` fell into `catch { generate a fresh one }`: a new id, which the boot binding check then answered with a reset and the same wipe. **Fix, at the file layer:** new [`electron/stateFile.ts`](electron/stateFile.ts) — `writeJsonAtomic` (temp file in the same folder → fsync → rename, retrying a Windows lock for about a second, else throwing with the old file intact) and `readJsonState` answering `present` / `absent` (no file only) / `corrupt`. Every userData JSON writer in `electron/` uses it (setup, id, auth secret, auto-start, warm routes, face roster, display, sync and update config, staged-update marker). **Boot:** *absent* keeps the owner's rule (wipe — a PC with no counter holds no data); *corrupt* sets the file aside and asks the replica which `pos_counters` row carries this PC's id — one row rebuilds the file and boot carries on, no row in a synced replica is the same "released" an intact file would get, anything else **holds** (no worker, no wipe, a warning dialog; the wizard offers this PC's counter and heals it). New [`electron/installationId.ts`](electron/installationId.ts): the id keeps a `.bak`, is minted only when the setup file is absent, and on a counter that has lost both copies is *null* — every check already reads that as "cannot tell"; never re-derived from the replica (two tills could end up sharing one id block). **Also:** the 5-minute onboarding timeout now resets only a replica that never synced — a filled one that could not finish a pull (offline after an upgrade) used to be wiped with its outbox. Narrative in [roadmap §4.7](docs/electron-offline-sync-roadmap.md). **Verified:** new `pnpm check:state-file` 25/25 in a temp folder — cut-short / empty / wrong-shape files read corrupt, a byte-order mark reads present, no temp file survives, a 300 ms PowerShell no-share lock is waited out and a 3 s one fails with the old file intact, and the five id cases; `tsc -p electron` and ESLint clean. **Owed:** a real-counter test after the next release (truncate `counter-setup.json` offline, restart, expect the same counter and the same unsent count).
- **2026-09-30 — Printing to a "Windows Printer" works again; the printer and COM lists no longer need `wmic`.** Batch 1, item 3 of the "find patches" sweep. **Bug:** [`thermal.js`](hardware-bridge/thermal.js) runs `print-raw.ps1` for every print to a USB printer on its Windows driver (Settings → Thermal Printer → Windows Printer), but the script lived in the old `tools/thermal-relay/` and was deleted with it on 2026-05-19 (`71e5c672`) — it never came across to the bridge, so every such print failed. The printer name was also pasted into a shell command string. **Fix:** the script is back as [`hardware-bridge/print-raw.ps1`](hardware-bridge/print-raw.ps1) (winspool RAW, unchanged), run with `execFile` and an argument array, so a name holding a quote or `$(…)` is passed as data — checked against a made-up name with both: the script answered `OpenPrinter failed for: <the exact name>` and ran nothing. [`verify-package`](scripts/verify-package.ts) now fails a build without it. The Windows printer list uses PowerShell `Get-CimInstance Win32_Printer` instead of `wmic` (gone from newer Windows 11); on this PC it listed the RETSOL TP806, the 80mm Series Printer and the 4BARCODE label printer. The COM-port lists in `thermal.js` and [`qr.js`](hardware-bridge/qr.js) drop their `wmic` fallback — `serialport` is packaged and checked, so a failure is now logged with its reason. `pnpm test:bridge` 14/14. **A real print on a Windows-driver printer is still owed** — needs a counter that uses one, after the next release.
- **2026-09-30 — Shop and e-commerce product queries are type-checked again.** Batch 1, item 8 of the "find patches" sweep. `src/shared/types/inventoryTypes.ts` wrapped `prisma.invProduct` in an `as unknown as` shim whose `where` / `data` / `include` were `Record<string, unknown>` — written before `prisma generate` knew the e-commerce columns, and its own header said to delete it afterwards. Fifteen shop, storefront and e-commerce files now use `prisma.invProduct` and the generated `InvProduct` type directly; tsc found nothing hiding behind it. **Looked at and left:** [`src/auth.d.ts`](src/auth.d.ts) has never taken effect — `next-auth` v5 only re-exports `Session` / `User` / `JWT` from `@auth/core`, which is not a direct dependency, so augmenting `"next-auth"` merges into nothing, and that is why every `session.user.<field>` read carries a cast. Making it real needs `@auth/core` as a direct dependency (or hoisted) and the augmentation moved to `@auth/core/types` — a dependency change, left for the owner.
- **2026-09-30 — The trading voucher's party card shows a company's balance.** Batch 1, item 7 of the "find patches" sweep. [`TradingVoucherPage`](src/modules/accountancy/components/TradingVoucherPage.tsx) skipped the call for a company ("Companies (B2B) — skip the call for now"), so a company always read ₹0, and a person's figure was the cached `Account.balance`. The card now reads [`/api/pos/party-balance/[accountId]`](src/app/api/pos/party-balance/[accountId]/route.ts) for any party — summed from the GL, the same read as the POS Rcv dialog and the credit gate, signed "due > 0 = owes us" (the sign the card already used) — and the address from the person or company record. Either request failing no longer blanks the other. **Live (2026-09-30):** picking the company "DNO , FSAS" reads `/api/pos/party-balance/10443` + `/api/companies/33` and shows **Due ₹4,300.00** with its address.
- **2026-09-30 — Linking a company to a person (or a contact to a company) shows at once.** Batch 1, item 6 of the "find patches" sweep. [`CustomerLinkPanel`](src/modules/customer/components/CustomerLinkPanel.tsx) and [`CompanyLinkPanel`](src/modules/customer/components/CompanyLinkPanel.tsx) copied `initialLinks` into their own state on first render, and the three customer pages bumped a `linkKey` counter to force a remount — but they bumped it *before* the refetch landed, so the panel re-read the old list and the new link did not appear; an unlink never told the parent. **Now the panels are controlled:** they draw the `links` prop and call `onChanged()` after a link or an unlink, and the parent refetches the detail. `linkKey` / `personLinkKey` / `companyLinkKey` are gone; on the All page the two "linked" handlers were exact copies of the detail refetch and now simply are it. The unused server components `PersonProfileView` / `CompanyProfileView` (the panels' only other users, imported nowhere) are deleted.
- **2026-09-30 — Optional Vouchers keeps its search and type filter when the dates change.** Batch 1, item 4 of the "find patches" sweep. [`optional-vouchers`](src/app/(protected)/office/accountancy/reports/optional-vouchers/page.tsx) re-ran `load(1, "", "All")` on every date change, so the request dropped the search and type the boxes still showed. **Now the request is built from state, not arguments:** one effect keyed on dates + debounced search + type + page; the page belongs to one set of filters and falls back to 1 when any changes (no second fetch); a slow old reply is discarded; the spinner is derived from "has this request answered", and a realtime refresh refetches the same view without it. The hand-rolled debounce timer and the suppressed lint line are gone. Pending Vouchers passes every filter through and was left alone. **Live (2026-09-30):** search `a` + type Sales Order, then From → 01/05/2026 sent one request, `from=2026-05-01…&search=a&type=SALES_ORDER`.
- **2026-09-30 — Dead leftovers removed.** Batch 1, item 9 of the "find patches" sweep: the `scripts/tmp-*` one-offs (each header said "delete once done"), `inspect-call-raw`, `survey-call-keys`, `drop-rguest.sql`, the retired no-op `src/scripts/backfill-universal-voucher.ts`, the unused `referenceStore`, and the always-empty `LEGACY_TO_SCOPED_ACTIONS` expansion in [`permissionScope.ts`](src/shared/lib/auth/permissionScope.ts). Comments that still promised a "conversion tombstone" (removed 2026-09-28) now say what the resolver does. `test-tally-date.ts` stays — it is a passing test, not a leftover. Nothing called any of them (no package script, no import).
- **2026-09-30 — Staff routes take a staff login, and the guard lint checks every handler, not every file.** From the "find patches" sweep (Batch 1, item 1). **Cause:** eight ticket / notification handlers checked only "is anyone signed in" with a bare `auth()` — [`tickets/[id]`](src/app/api/tickets/[id]/route.ts) GET + PUT, [`participants`](src/app/api/tickets/[id]/participants/route.ts) GET, [`notes`](src/app/api/tickets/[id]/notes/route.ts), [`transfer`](src/app/api/tickets/[id]/transfer/route.ts), [`bill-draft`](src/app/api/tickets/[id]/bill-draft/route.ts), [`shipping-slip`](src/app/api/tickets/[id]/shipping-slip/route.ts) and [`notifications/[id]`](src/app/api/notifications/[id]/route.ts) — so a signed-in **portal customer** could read and edit any repair ticket, add notes signed with a staff name, and mark staff notifications read; they also skipped the released-counter stop. [`GET /api/retention/protection`](src/app/api/retention/protection/route.ts) had **no guard at all**. The lint missed them because it counted `auth(` as a guard and checked per **file**, so one guarded POST vouched for an open GET beside it. **Fix:** all of them use `requireAuth()` (Protection read takes the same permission as its write); [`check-guard-lint`](scripts/check-guard-lint.ts) no longer accepts `auth(`, and checks **each exported handler** — a handler is guarded when its own body names a guard or calls a same-file helper that does. Proved to trip on a scratch route with a guarded POST and a bare-`auth()` GET. The two constant "retired" handlers (`accountancy/journal-entries` POST 410, `settings/couriers` POST 405) are gone — Next answers 405 for a missing method — along with the Couriers / Standard Courier screens' dead "add" calls to them (the six couriers are created on first read). **Noticed, not changed:** [`api/calls`](src/app/api/calls/route.ts) POST accepts every request when `CALLS_API_KEY` is unset — owner's call. No schema, seed or permission change; tsc and guard-lint clean. **Live (2026-09-30):** without a session, `GET /api/retention/protection`, the ticket, participants and notification routes answer 401; the two removed POSTs answer 405.
- **2026-09-30 — The Role page can give and take every tick, and a Delete tick is always an Edit tick.** Step 4 of the Role-page audit. **(1) Five ticks had no box** — `hr:staff:edit`, `hr:attendance:edit`, `hr:leave:approve`, `hr:payroll:run` and `settings:manage` could only arrive by "Select all" or copy-from-role and never be unticked one by one (the `hr-staff` matrix row was never rendered). They are now yes/no boxes beside their link in [`SIDEBAR_LINK_EXTRA_TOGGLE`](src/modules/admin/lib/roleMatrixConfig.tsx) — Staff Directory "Can edit staff records", Attendance *and* Calendar & Hours "Can mark attendance & holidays" (one key), Leave "Can approve leave", Payroll "Can run payroll", Backup & Restore "Can back up & restore" (`settings:manage` now gates only backup / restore and the repair ticket's dev override). `idsUnlockedByLinks` makes each savable wherever its HR / Settings link is pooled, so an HR department's roles can finally be given them. The dead `hr-staff` row is gone. **(2) The ladder invented ticks.** [`crudLadder.ts`](src/shared/lib/auth/crudLadder.ts) read any id ending in `:edit` as a create / edit / delete set, so ticking "Can change tax" added a `compliance:tax:create` that does not exist and Save warned "not seeded" — and the new HR boxes would have done the same. The resource ladder now applies only to a base with a real `:create` permission (`CRUD_BASES`, from `PERMISSIONS`). **(3) Delete did not count as Edit** on the repair ticket page (`canEditTicket`) and the user page's tabs (`isAdmin` on `users:edit`) — both read `includes()` while the session carries only the top rung; now `hasPermissionWithScope`. **(4) The Quick Actions "POS +" chip** granted `pos:shift:open` without close, and read off while "Start / End shift" read on. `QUICK_ACTION_OPTIONS` entries now carry `ids`; the POS one takes the Start / End pair, so both chips flip open + close together.
- **2026-09-29 — A link to another page gives way when the page lock would refuse it.** Follow-up to the page lock below. Owner: the E-Commerce product form's "Reorder" link sent an R3 cashier (E-Commerce → Products, no Inventory) to "no access" — and *"the desktop app has no address bar, so links on other screens are the only way a cashier reaches a page"*; sweep them all. **Mechanism:** new [`GatedLink`](src/shared/components/GatedLink.tsx) — a `Link` that renders only when `useCanOpenPage()` allows its href, else nothing (`whenLocked="hide"`, a button-like link) or plain text (`"text"`, a name or a link inside a sentence, with its own `textClassName`); `pageLinkIds` now ignores a `?query` on the address. **Hidden / made plain (27 files by swap):** the product form's Reorder and Update-prices links; the attribute-masters link in the item form; Org Chart → Positions; GST classification → product groups; BI Stock Health ↔ FSN and → Reorder; Contacts → repair ticket; e-com order → repair ticket; Stock Summary → Item Ledger; POS Counters → shift; Costing → Org Defaults; Device Location and the Repair dashboard → tickets; Couriers → Standard Courier; Downloads ↔ Thermal Printer / QR Display / the bridge notice; the user page's "All Users"; the deal dialog's customer; the e-com dashboard's queue / product links; Home's "Open ticket"; attendance → Employee Activity; payroll → staff; staff profile → ledger; activity timeline → ticket; party names on Item Ledger, the voucher registers, Outstandings, Pending / Optional vouchers; Inbox message links. **Click handlers:** Balance Sheet / Trial Balance drills (stock summary, P&L, ledger, group; a party row falls back to its ledger without Contacts); Print labels ↔ Update prices; the voucher menu's Print labels; the GST hub cards; SL Search (`RowLink` → `GatedLink`: a name stays as text, "Open" goes); breadcrumbs (a locked crumb is text — CRM's root is Deals); the notification bell (a locked target opens the Inbox); Work Flow items (no link). **Exits after a save / error / delete** go somewhere openable: POS sale / order / estimate edit → the office register or else `/vouchers/pos`; the trading voucher form → Item Ledger or else Work Flow; `LifecycleActions` and the Journal voucher → the register or else Work Flow; the Repair + button opens the new ticket only when the person may. **Left as they are:** same-tick links (list ↔ detail, a module's own area), the Board / Deals / Help `router.replace` that only clears `?new` on their own page, Stock Summary's "Back to Balance Sheet" (shown only after coming from there), the storefront's placeholder ad (public site), and the Accountancy report drill helpers `groupReportUrl` / `accountLedgerUrl` outside the Balance Sheet and Trial Balance (Accountancy-to-Accountancy; no live role holds part of Accountancy). `CompanyProfileView`, `PersonProfileView` and the assignments `TicketCard` are imported nowhere.
- **2026-09-29 — Every office page opens only on its own Role-page sidebar tick.** Step 3 of the Role-page audit. The sidebar already hid an unticked link, but a typed address, a bookmark or a link on another screen still opened the page: only 14 of the 131 links were locked per link (HR, Retention, Help Desk); Accountancy, CRM, E-Commerce, Administration and Pricing Strategy opened module-wide on any one tick; BI, Inventory, Contacts lists, Repair, POS Shift, Org Chart and all 21 Settings pages had no gate at all. **Fix:** one rule, [`pageAccess.ts`](src/shared/lib/auth/pageAccess.ts), built from the nav tree (`sidebarModules.ts` rows now carry `paths`), enforced in [`proxy.ts`](src/proxy.ts) off the session it already resolves — no extra read. A path takes the most specific sidebar link it sits under (its href, or a new `pathPrefixes` on the nav item: Vouchers Register owns every register tab, Cash / Bank Book own the `reports/cash-book` / `bank-book` views the top bar opens), else its module's area (hubs, shared drill-ins → any tick in the module, the old rule), else it is open; Home, Work Flow, Inbox, `/office/me`, `/office/user/*` are always open. A refusal lands on Work Flow with `?noaccess=1` and [`NoAccessNotice`](src/shared/components/NoAccessNotice.tsx) says why (the permission layouts use the same flag now). **Screens that list pages follow it** (`canOpenPage` / new [`useCanOpenPage`](src/shared/hooks/useCanOpenPage.ts)): the Financial Insights, BI and both Inventory hubs, both Masters tab strips, the Finance top bar (a figure stays but links only when openable), the Home cards (new client [`QuickLink`](src/modules/home/QuickLink.tsx)), the sidebar's module name (opens the first page the person can open), and the Accountancy Masters and Retention index redirects. **Removed:** the module-wide guards in the Accountancy (whole layout — it also wrongly refused a Compliance-only person the GST pages under its address), CRM ×3, E-Commerce, Administration and Pricing Strategy layouts — six session reads per page load. **Bugs fixed on the way:** the Masters tab strip read the CRUD ladder with `includes()` (a Delete holder lost Opening Balance); the Retention index preferred sections by Edit with `includes()` and checked Spend Tiers against `customers:edit` instead of `tiers:edit`. **Measured against the live roles:** all 130 link pages open on their own tick; R1 holds all 131 link ticks; R2 Branch Manager loses only E-Commerce → Ads (unticked); R3 Counter Operator loses ~60 pages it could reach but never had in its sidebar — Settings except Downloads / QR / Screen Size / Thermal Printer, all of BI and Inventory, POS Shift Reports / Counters, Org Chart, Recurring Tasks. If cashiers used Inventory → Products or Item Ledger to look up stock, tick those for R3 (the Sales department must pool Inventory first). Unchanged: `/vouchers/**` and print pages (voucher ticks guard their writes), the Admin pages' extra create/edit/delete requirement.
- **2026-09-29 — The Home page's Finance card opens real pages.** From the same audit: [`FinanceBriefing`](src/modules/home/DeptBriefings.tsx) linked `/office/accountancy/daybook`, `/receivables`, `/cash-bank` and `/gst`, none of which exist, so four of its six tiles gave "page not found". Each tile now uses the sidebar's own address (`nav.ts`): Day Book → `reports/day-book`, Receivables → `reports/receivables`, GST Hub → `reports/gst`. "Cash & Bank Book" became two tiles, **Cash Book** (`cash-ledgers`) and **Bank Book** (`bank-ledgers`), as the sidebar has them; the "Journals" tile went, because it pointed at `/office/accountancy`, which redirects to the Day Book — the first tile again. A scan of every hard-coded page address in `src` found no other dead link (the rest it flagged are path prefixes, an unused `basePath`, and commented-out roadmap entries).
- **2026-09-29 — "Can login outside counter" is enforced by the server; the office website is for office staff only.** From the same audit: the tick lived only in the [login page](src/app/staff/login/page.tsx), which checked it *after* the session cookie was issued, so posting a right password straight to the NextAuth callback gave a working session from any machine. Owner: *"we retire the legacy bridge since the electron app working well so the web become admin/higher permission holder only"*. **Fix:** [`authorizeStaffCredentials`](src/shared/lib/auth/authorizeStaff.ts) now refuses a right password when this is not a counter and the person lacks `auth:login:outside-counter`, before the session exists, throwing `OutsideCounterSignin` (a `CredentialsSignin` with code `outside-counter`, shared through [`session.ts`](src/shared/lib/constants/session.ts)) so the page says why instead of "Invalid email or password". "A counter" is new [`isThisPcACounter()`](src/shared/lib/counter/releaseGuard.ts): never off the desktop app (the office website, the dev server); on it, the setup file — the same test the login page makes, not `IS_COUNTER_PC` (false on a till whose local database did not start), and an unreadable file counts as a counter so a till is never locked out on one bad read. The page's own after-sign-in check stays for the one case the server reads as a counter: a released PC whose file is not cleared yet. **Legacy bridge retired from login:** the website no longer asks the local bridge whether it sits at a counter (`isWebCounterBound` deleted) — it is never a counter. The PIN path was already counter-only. Web sessions of people without the tick that were open before the deploy run out on their own (6 h lifetime). Known and unchanged: a browser on the shop LAN opening a counter's own server (`:786`) is on that counter.
- **2026-09-29 — Four more voucher writes now respect their Role-page tick.** Owner asked *"is the rule page fully respected?"*; a read-only audit of every tick (vouchers, sidebar links, action ticks) found the answer is no, and the owner approved step 1 — the voucher holes. **(1) Journal Cancel / Restore** ([`journal-entries/[id]/cancel`](src/app/api/accountancy/journal-entries/[id]/cancel/route.ts), [`/restore`](src/app/api/accountancy/journal-entries/[id]/restore/route.ts)) checked the fixed Journal key and never read the voucher, so a Journal-cancel holder could cancel a Sale, Payment or POS bill by id; both now load the type and take `userCanVoucher(type, "cancel")`, like their post / hard-delete siblings. **(2) Inventory create** ([`POST /api/inventory/vouchers`](src/app/api/inventory/vouchers/route.ts)) never validated `type`, so a kind outside both matrices (`ONLINE_INVOICE`, OPENING, junk) passed every gate and could be posted; it now refuses anything but the office inventory kinds and POS Sale / Order / Estimate. **(3) Opening bills** — [`PUT accounts/[id]/opening-bills`](src/app/api/accountancy/accounts/[id]/opening-bills/route.ts) now takes Accountancy Masters Edit or Contacts Delete (the ticks of the one-figure editors beside it), except the same-figure split `settleFromPicks` makes when a Receipt / Payment settles a one-figure opening, so the counter keeps working; the bulk [`opening-balance/to-bills`](src/app/api/accountancy/opening-balance/to-bills/route.ts) needs Masters Edit to convert (the preview stays open). **(4) Posting a POS draft** ([`/[id]/post`](src/app/api/inventory/vouchers/[id]/post/route.ts)) passed with no tick; new `userCanPostVoucher` in [`permissions.ts`](src/shared/lib/auth/permissions.ts) sends POS Sale / Order / Estimate to their POS-row Create tick, every other kind to its Post tick, and the create route uses it too. **Still open from the audit:** several screens show buttons without the tick. (The Home page's Finance card, the missing Role-page boxes and the `includes()` ladder reads were fixed the same day — see above.)
- **2026-09-29 — Every voucher now respects its Role-page tick: office edit, Journal edit / post, POS Rcv / Exp / Return, Duplicate, Convert.** Owner, after the POS fix below: *"any more voucher not respecting role page tick check"* → approved all four fixes. A sweep of every voucher write route found five gaps and two small ones; everything else (office create / post / cancel / delete, GL voucher edit, Journal create / cancel / delete, payroll) already checked its tick. **(1) Office voucher edit** — [`PATCH /api/inventory/vouchers/[id]`](src/app/api/inventory/vouchers/[id]/route.ts) (Sale, Purchase, returns, GRN, Challan, Physical Stock, Branch Transfer, MJ, Rejection In / Out, Estimate, Purchase Order — posted ones re-post stock and GL) checked only sign-in; it now takes the kind's **Cancel / edit** tick for every kind, draft or posted, the rule the GL voucher edit already had. The Day Book menu and the Inventory Details menu ([`VoucherActionsMenu`](src/modules/inventory/components/VoucherActionsMenu.tsx)) hide Edit / Cancel / Delete by the same function for every kind. **(2 + 3) Journal edit and post** ([`journal-entries/[id]`](src/app/api/accountancy/journal-entries/[id]/route.ts), [`/post`](src/app/api/accountancy/journal-entries/[id]/post/route.ts)) took "any Accountancy link"; now `userCanVoucher(type, "cancel" | "post")`, like the GL voucher routes. **(4) POS Rcv / Exp** ([`receipts`](src/app/api/pos/shifts/[id]/receipts/route.ts), [`expenditures`](src/app/api/pos/shifts/[id]/expenditures/route.ts)) took the Cash drop chip and **(5) the with-bill Sale Return** ([`pos/return`](src/app/api/pos/return/route.ts)) the Start / End shift chip; all three now `requirePosVoucher(<kind>, "create")` — the tick `usePosAccess` already used to show the tab, so screen and server agree. The open-shift rule is unchanged. **Small ones:** [`duplicate`](src/app/api/inventory/vouchers/[id]/duplicate/route.ts) now needs the kind's Create tick, and the create route's check became POS-aware, so Convert Estimate → POS Order needs the POS Order Create tick. **One function** — `canActOnVoucher(type, action, perms)` in [`posVoucherMatrix.ts`](src/shared/lib/auth/posVoucherMatrix.ts), browser-safe, now taking create as well — is the rule for both menus and, through `userCanActOnVoucher` (renamed from `userCanChangeVoucher`), for the server. **Who loses anything (seed snapshot):** R1 nothing; R2 nothing (holds Cancel / edit on every kind and journal); R3 loses office Sale edit, which it cannot reach (no Accountancy links), and keeps Rcv / Exp / Return (holds those Create ticks). Owner will give R3 office UI later — at that point edit is a Role-page tick (Sale → Cancel / edit), no code change. Still deliberately open: the register's Draft / Issued toggle, online orders (E-Commerce module), system vouchers (shift variance, gift-card issue), bill-wise linking. No schema, seed or permission change. tsc, ESLint, guard-lint and doc-sync clean; `canActOnVoucher` run against the seeded role ticks gives R1 everything, R2 create + edit / cancel but no delete on every kind, R3 create only, and all three keep the Rcv / Exp / Return tabs. **Not driven signed in** — same reasons as below; owner to try an R2 / R3 login.
- **2026-09-29 — POS Sale, Order and Estimate: Edit / Cancel / Delete now obey the Role page's POS table.** Owner: *"pos estimate and pos order are not gated to restrict delete edit"*, then a screenshot of R2 Branch Manager with Delete unticked on every POS row — *"how can branch manager can delete without permission?"*. **Cause:** the POS table's Cancel / edit and Delete ticks (`pos:voucher:cancel|delete:<kind>`) were seeded and shown but read by nothing; only Create was checked. The Day Book's cancel / hard-delete routes used `userCanVoucherKind`, which answers "allowed" for any type with no office voucher-matrix row — and `POS`, `POS_ORDER`, `POS_ESTIMATE` have none. POS Sale Return / Receipt / Expense were never open (they save as `SALE_RETURN` / `RECEIPT` / `PAYMENT`, which the office matrix gates). **Fix:** [`posVoucherMatrix.ts`](src/shared/lib/auth/posVoucherMatrix.ts) now knows which saved type each POS row owns (`savedAs` + `posKindForVoucherType`), and new `userCanActOnVoucher` in [`permissions.ts`](src/shared/lib/auth/permissions.ts) sends those three types to `canDoPosVoucher` (POS tick OR the office-equivalent key, ladder honoured) and everything else to `userCanVoucherKind` as before. Used by the inventory **cancel**, **restore** (un-cancel), **hard-delete** and **draft DELETE** routes, by `userCanRestoreAudit` (Recycle Bin list + restore), and by the two edit saves — [`PATCH /api/inventory/vouchers/[id]`](src/app/api/inventory/vouchers/[id]/route.ts) (POS kinds only; office kinds keep their page-level gate) and [`PATCH /api/orders/inv/[id]`](src/app/api/orders/[source]/[id]/route.ts). The [Day Book 3-dot menu](src/modules/accountancy/components/DayBookActionsMenu.tsx) hides Edit / Cancel / Delete on these rows by the same rule; the Day Book stays the one place to delete (owner's call — no delete on the POS screen). **Bug found on the way:** the order edit route was gated by `requireModuleAccess("orders")`, but no `orders` sidebar module exists (it is E-Commerce → Orders), so saving an edited POS Order / Estimate — and an office Sales Order at Review — was refused for everyone except the env super-admin; it now takes the kind's Cancel / edit tick. **Draft DELETE** (`DELETE /api/inventory/vouchers/[id]`, no screen calls it, skips the Recycle Bin) was requireAuth-only for every kind; it now takes the Delete tick like hard-delete, so flipping an estimate to Draft is not a way round. Deliberately untouched: the bill-wise link check (`allocations`, still `userCanVoucherKind`) and the register's Draft / Issued toggle. No schema, seed or permission change — the ticks already existed. tsc, ESLint, guard-lint and doc-sync clean; the rule run against the seeded role ticks gives R1 all, R2 edit + cancel but no delete, R3 none, matching the Role page. **Not driven signed in** — no dev server and the pane is not signed in, and the dev server writes to live, so no real voucher was cancelled or deleted as a test; owner to try an R2 / R3 login. Reaches the office with the `d_office` deploy and the counters with the next release.
- **2026-09-29 — Back never lands on the faces / PIN screen while signed in.** Owner, on Counter 23 (Rakesh, id 93): the office **Sales voucher's** Back / Close went to the lock screen; *"after uninstall - reinstall solved"*. **Cause:** every voucher's Back is `router.back()` whenever the window has history, and the sign-in screen was in that history. The [login page](src/app/staff/login/page.tsx) entered the app with `router.push`, so the faces screen sat right behind the first page after sign-in; and a session that ended with a voucher open ([`SessionWrapper`](src/shared/components/SessionWrapper.tsx) `goLogin`) sent the till to sign-in with `callbackUrl` = that voucher, so after the PIN the voucher reopened with the lock screen directly behind it (and a stale copy of itself behind that). A reinstall — or a plain restart — only started the history fresh. On the counter app the shell (`resources/shell/login.html`) was also behind `/staff/login`, so enough Backs reached the shell's faces screen from any page. **Fix:** (1) the login page leaves with `router.replace` everywhere — `enter()` (PIN, password, choose-PIN), the drawer takeover's Accept, and the new-PC `/setup/counter` hop; (2) `goLogin` uses `location.replace`, so the lock screen takes the dead page's place and Back after the PIN goes to the screen before the voucher; (3) [`main.ts`](electron/main.ts) `loadApp` runs `navigationHistory.clear()` once the app page has loaded. Deliberate Sign out (`signOutTo`, `assign` on purpose) and the idle sign-out are unchanged; no voucher screen touched. tsc, ESLint and `electron:compile` clean; an Electron 43 probe confirmed the clear (history 2 → 1, `canGoBack` false) and that Back from a voucher reaches the page before it and stops there. **Not driven signed in** (the pane is not signed in). (1)+(2) reach the office site with the `d_office` deploy and the counters with the next release; (3) is counter-release only.

- **2026-09-29 — Typing an IFSC fills Bank Name and Branch by itself, on all three bank forms.** Owner, on a company's Bank tab: *"can we have ifsc api to fill branch"*, then *"for wrong information manual override"*. New [`/api/ifsc/[code]`](src/app/api/ifsc/[code]/route.ts) (signed-in staff) asks Razorpay's free IFSC service — their mirror of RBI's list, no key — the same server-side proxy shape as `/api/shop/pincode`, and answers `{ bank, branch, address, district, state }` or `{ ok:false }` for a code not in the list. Cached a month on the server (a branch's details barely move), a day in the browser, and once per code per session in the hook, so retyping or reopening a party never asks twice. One hook, [`useIfscLookup`](src/shared/hooks/useIfscLookup.ts), and one hint line, [`IfscLookupHint`](src/shared/components/IfscLookupHint.tsx), serve the party **Bank** tab ([PartyBankTab.tsx](src/modules/customer/components/PartyBankTab.tsx)), the staff salary account ([StaffPersonalForm.tsx](src/modules/hr/components/StaffPersonalForm.tsx)) and the bank-ledger particulars in Create / Edit Ledger ([LedgerParticularsFields.tsx](src/modules/accountancy/components/LedgerParticularsFields.tsx), whose IFSC box now upper-cases too). **Manual override is the rule:** the lookup writes Bank Name / Branch only into a blank box or one still holding its own earlier value, so anything staff typed stays; and it writes only after the IFSC is *typed* — opening a saved record never changes it. The hint under IFSC says what RBI's list has (*"HDFC Bank · Panbazar — Guwahati, Assam"*) and offers **Use** when the form says otherwise. A code missing from the list shows an amber note but never blocks a save (a new branch can be newer than the list); offline, nothing fills and nothing breaks. RBI's upper-case branch / district / state are proper-cased; bank names arrive mixed-case and are kept. No schema, seed or permission change.
- **2026-09-29 — A Receipt / Payment with bills ticked no longer fails "has only ₹X open — cannot place ₹2X".** Owner, on Counter 94: PMT-940789-26 saved and posted, then *"PMT-940789-26 has only ₹5038.00 open — cannot place ₹10076.00."* Two links ran on one save: [`/api/accountancy/vouchers`](src/app/api/accountancy/vouchers/route.ts) POST auto-links a posted Receipt / Payment oldest-first (`autoLinkSettlement`, since ff43defd — its comment assumed the office form had no bill picker), then the form's [`settleFromPicks`](src/modules/accountancy/lib/settleFromPicks.ts) read what each ticked pair already stood at — the server's link — and asked for that PLUS the tick. When the auto-link picked other bills than the ticks, the ticks were lost and the refusal read "only ₹0.00 open". On a counter it came and went with how fast the replica saw the server's link (R:Local reads). Fix: [`GlVoucherPage`](src/modules/accountancy/components/GlVoucherPage.tsx) sends `autoLink: false` whenever it will settle ticks itself (no ticks → the server auto-links as before), and `settleFromPicks` sends the voucher-just-posted's ticks as the pair totals — only an older credit's pair keeps what it already had — so a new counter build is right against the current server too. The failing request is refused whole, so nothing was double-linked; affected vouchers keep the auto-link's placement. No migration; counter build and `d_office` deploy in either order.
- **2026-09-29 — Branch address switch works on every voucher print; Settings shows it only where it prints.** The switch only printed where the sample carried `branchAddress` — the POS bill and Receipt / Payment. Now [`voucherReceiptSample.ts`](src/modules/accountancy/lib/voucherReceiptSample.ts) fills it in all four builders from the voucher's `UniversalVoucher.branch` (one `branchAddressSelect` + `branchAddressText` → `formatStructuredAddressBlock`, selected inside each builder's own voucher query, no extra round trip; Receipt / Payment drop their separate branch lookup): `buildInvSample` (Sale, Purchase, returns, orders, GRN, Challan, estimates, stock vouchers), `buildGlSample` (Contra, Bank Deposit, Cash Handover, Credit / Debit Note, the party Bank Deposit Slip), `buildJeSample` (Journal). A4, 80mm page and the silent bridge (`printHeader`) all read the same sample — no bridge change. Settings: one list, `NO_BRANCH_ADDRESS_KINDS` in [`voucherTemplateSamples.ts`](src/modules/settings/lib/voucherTemplateSamples.ts) (Repair Ticket, Shipping Slip, both labels, Coupon, Gift Card, Payslip — their prints carry no branch), drives both the preview sample (every other kind now previews the sample address, not just Sale / POS / Online Invoice) and the switch's visibility. **Shift Close** always printed its shift's branch but Settings hid the switch — now shown; its page uses `formatStructuredAddressBlock` instead of a hand-built copy. A branch with only the legacy one-line `Branch.address` still prints nothing here (same as the POS bill); `formatBranchAddressBlock` has that fallback if wanted. Seed snapshot has the switch off on all 33 templates; **live not checked** — any kind with it on starts printing the branch address once this ships.

- **2026-09-29 — Receipt / Payment print as ONE slip, from every button, on its print template.** Owner: *"print balance in receipt voucher too"* → *"merge properly it should 100% respect print template"*. A Receipt had **two builders**: `paymentSlip.ts` (Day Book menu, POS Rcv / Exp, the POS overpayment RV-…, silent thermal) had the party balance; `buildGlSample` (the form's **Print after save**, and the fallback page when the printer did not answer) had the UPI / cheque details but **never worked out the balance**, so the A4 print from the Receipt form printed no balance even with the template's Party balance on. Now: (1) one builder, `buildMoneySample` in [`voucherReceiptSample.ts`](src/modules/accountancy/lib/voucherReceiptSample.ts) — `paymentSlip.ts` deleted; `buildGlSample` hands every RECEIPT / PAYMENT to it except a cash Payment into a party's bank (Bank Deposit Slip, unchanged). (2) One page: `/receipt/payment/[id]` only **redirects** to `/receipt/voucher/gl/[id]` (old links, bookmarks and older counter builds keep working); `receiptHref` and `PosScreen` point straight there. (3) One bridge payload, `buildVoucherBridgePayload`, for `/api/print/thermal/[source]/[id]` and the POS sale response. (4) Template: the **Branch address** switch works on Receipt / Payment (the slip now carries the voucher's branch address; it never did); Settings hides **HSN tax analysis table** and **MRP (struck)** for Receipt / Payment — a money slip has no goods, so they did nothing; the Settings preview sample shows a named party + "HDFC Bank · UPI (4532)", not Walk-in / Cash (a walk-in has no balance). (5) The slip's date is the voucher date only — `UniversalVoucher.date` is `@db.Date`, so the old date-time print showed a made-up **05:30** (00:00 on Vercel). (6) **Bug:** `partyBalanceAroundVoucher` subtracted the voucher's own lines from the POSTED total, so a **DRAFT** printed from the form showed "before" shifted by its own amount; "before" is now everything else, "after" = before + this voucher — identical for posted vouchers. POS Rcv = RECEIPT, Exp = PAYMENT: same templates as the office forms. `personal.local.json` Receipt Party balance → on/on (the DB already had it on). The **Branch address** switch now works on every other voucher print too (see the entry above); the slip's fixed wording ("Received with thanks from…") is code, not template — left as is (editable wording would need new template columns + a migration).

- **2026-09-29 — Every counter save offers Save or Save + Print; Ctrl+Enter is always the print one.** Owner: *"in pos/counter all voucher should have choice to print or not print. and same ctrl+enter to save+print"*, then *"save estimate, save and print estimate"*. New [`SavePrintButtons`](src/modules/pos/components/SavePrintButtons.tsx) + `savePrintKey` are the one pair of CTAs and the one key rule: Enter → save without printing (only where the dialog binds it), Ctrl/Cmd+Enter → save + print, and a held key's repeats never save (the Ctrl+Enter that opens a checkout must not also confirm it). [`CheckoutDialog`](src/modules/pos/components/CheckoutDialog.tsx) uses it and gains a **`confirm` mode** — title + the two CTAs, no tenders, the dialog itself takes the keys. **Estimate** now confirms there like a Sale: Ctrl+Enter / the button opens it, *Save Estimate* (↵) / *Save & Print Estimate* (Ctrl ↵) — it used to save + print in one go. An **Order / Estimate edit** confirms the same way (*Update … / Update & Print …*, the print fires before the page navigates; edits never printed before). **Exp**: *Record* / *Record + Print*, Enter in Amount or Note (the ledger picker keeps its own Enter) — it used to print every time. **Return**: *Return · ₹X* / *Return + Print* prints the SALE_RETURN slip (`returnVoucherId` was already in the API answer) — it never printed before; plain Enter stays with the +/- steppers and the reason box. **Shift close**: *Close & sweep to vault* / *Close + Print*, Ctrl+Enter only (closing posts GL); the "Print after save" tick is gone (`pos_shift_print_after_save` is no longer read), the Thermal tick stays for the paper. Sale, Order and Rcv already worked this way. Also: the Sales register's Orders / Estimates printer icon opened A4 with the browser dialog — now `printVoucherThermal`; the Return toast no longer ends in "· JE undefined" (the API never sent `journalEntryRef`). **Sale edit** followed once `PosScreen.tsx` was free: *Update Sale* now opens the checkout in `confirm` mode — *Update Sale* (↵) / *Update & Print Sale* (Ctrl ↵) — and prints like the register reprint (`printVoucherThermal("inv", id, "POS")`, the slip rebuilt from the saved voucher). Lint + tsc clean; not browser-verified (the pane is not signed in).

- **2026-09-29 — POS Estimate and Order print on the thermal printer.** Owner: *"the pos estimate not going to thermal"*. [`PosQuoteScreen`](src/modules/pos/components/PosQuoteScreen.tsx) was missed by the 2026-09-07 silent-print audit: Save Estimate / Save + Print opened `/receipt/voucher/inv/[id]` with no `?mode`, so `resolvePaperFormat` fell to **A4** and auto-fired the browser dialog — the POS_ESTIMATE / POS_ORDER templates' 80mm default never had a say. Both now go through `printVoucherThermal("inv", id, "POS_ESTIMATE" | "POS_ORDER")`, like Rcv / Exp: bridge + printer configured → silent slip from the kind's thermal template; otherwise the format-selection page on the 80mm view, nothing auto-printed. An Order's advance RV-… follows as `printVoucherThermal("gl", id, "RECEIPT")`. Still to do: a counter test of the real slip.

- **2026-09-29 — POS bills print Balance due like a Sale.** Owner: *"balance due should be printed on pos too like sale"*. Four gaps closed.
