- **2026-07-22 — POS shipping charge now carries GST (composite-supply rule)**: the counter-typed shipping amount stays **tax-INCLUSIVE** (customer pays exactly what the cashier typed; no bill total moved), but the embedded GST is now computed, stored, and booked. **Rate rule** (`resolveShippingGstRate` in [stock.ts](src/modules/inventory/lib/stock.ts), used by client AND server): the GST-rate group holding **>50% of the bill's pre-tax, pre-discount goods value** wins; a mixed bill (no group >50%, ties included) takes the **max rate** on the bill; a **service-only bill** applies the same rule over its service rates (repair @18% → shipping @18%); all-exempt/empty → 0%. **Storage:** new `VoucherDetail.shippingTaxAmt` (additive, default 0; `shippingAmt` stays GROSS). **GL** (`buildInvVoucherGlLines`): Shipping Income leg posts **NET** (`shippingAmt − shippingTaxAmt`); the tax joins the existing `chargesTax` pool onto the CGST/SGST/IGST legs — every non-POS caller passes nothing → 0 → byte-identical GL (ecommerce/manual vouchers/purchases/returns untouched; POS returns never carry shipping). **POS screen:** "incl. GST @n% · ₹x" hint beside the Shipping input (display only — server recomputes; no payload change). **Receipt fix (same commit):** the printed CGST/SGST box now pools line tax + **service-charge GST** (was silently goods-only — pre-existing gap) + shipping GST, so the printed tax always equals the books. Gates: tsc clean; resolver harness 8/8 PASS (dominance/tie/fallback/exempt + inclusive back-calc 118→100+18). _Additive schema — needs `pnpm prisma db push` (Claude can't). Parked: ecommerce checkout shipping still untaxed (own change later); estimates/orders inherit at conversion time since /api/pos/sale recomputes._
- **2026-07-18 — POS Services: sell services like products (Settings-defined catalogue, per-service income ledger + GST)**: replaces the bad-UX "+ Add charge" path for service income (kept as-is for odd rows). New **`PosService`** table (`prisma/schema/pos.prisma`: name unique, `accountId` → INCOME-group ledger, `gstRateId` → GstRate, optional tax-inclusive `defaultPricePaise`, sortOrder, isActive) + **Settings → POS Services** page (`/office/settings/pos-services`, [`PosServicesManager`](src/modules/settings/components/PosServicesManager.tsx), CRUD `/api/settings/pos-services[/[id]]` — income-group validated; GST dropdown defaults to the 18% rate). **POS UI:** active services (`GET /api/pos/services`) are added from a **"+ Add service charge" dropdown button in the cart column (Sale mode only)** — NOT the product search box (services have no barcode, so the scan/type dropdown stays products-only). Picking one makes a `kind:"service"` CartLine (`id = −serviceId`, negative so every productId-keyed setter still works); cart renders service rows **after product rows** with a sky tint + Service badge + "GST n%" in place of MRP/Barcode; price entry is tax-inclusive like products. The old raw-ledger "+ Add charge" (GlAccountCombobox) UI was **removed** — the button now adds catalogue services, not raw ledgers. Shipping + Round-off are an always-visible flex row (Adjustments toggle removed); `b.charges` plumbing stays dormant. **Posting (`/api/pos/sale`):** new `services[]` payload (id/qty/tax-EXCLUSIVE unitPrice; server re-resolves ledger + rate — never trusts the client); each becomes a **TAX-BEARING charge** — `ChargeInput.taxAmount` added to `settlementJson` ([settlement.ts](src/shared/lib/accountancy/settlement.ts)) and `buildInvVoucherGlLines` pools `Σ charge.taxAmount` into the CGST/SGST/IGST split while the charge leg posts the NET to the income ledger (additive — no charge carries tax ⇒ byte-identical GL, parity safe). `chargesNet` counts service GROSS so totals/JE balance; services-only sales allowed (`lines` may be empty); services excluded from loyalty earn/redeem base + coupon eval on BOTH sides; stock arm never sees them. Edit round-trip preserves `taxAmount` (vouchers/[id] GET/PATCH + PosScreen editMeta). Order/Estimate **guarded** (toast: Sale-only for now — TODO(pos-services) bridge in PosQuoteScreen). Gates: tsc clean, ESLint clean. _Additive schema — needs `pnpm prisma db push` (Claude can't); not run-tested in the browser (table pending push + auth). Known display note: the web receipt (`/receipt/invoice/[id]`) shows a service as a charge row ("Service: X"), the POS screen/thermal show it as a line. - **2026-07-17 — Auto credit-limit calculator (Settings → Credit Limit)**: `Account.creditLimit` (manual, per-customer) now has an optional **auto floor** layered on top. New singleton **`CreditLimitConfig`** (id=1, `prisma/schema/accounts.prisma`): `autoEnabled` (master toggle, default **OFF**), `windowDays` (X, default 90), `pctOfSales` (Y, default 10), optional `maxAutoLimit` + `roundToStep`. **Auto limit = Y% of the customer's POSTED SALE/POS sales in the last X days**; the **effective limit enforced everywhere = max(manual, auto)** — a manual limit never lowers a good auto number and vice-versa. When auto is OFF everything collapses to the old deny-by-default (manual only, null/0 = cash-only). New resolver `src/shared/lib/accountancy/creditLimit.ts` (`computeAutoCreditLimit` / `windowSalesTotal` / `loadCreditLimitConfig` / `resolveEffectiveCreditLimit`); `checkCreditLimit` (POS sale + order fulfil) and the POS `account-snapshot` now resolve through it. **`loadCreditLimitConfig` swallows a missing-table error → auto-OFF**, a temporary bridge so the pre-`db push` window can't 500 POS sales / the Contacts page (remove once the table is live). New **Settings → Credit Limit** page (`/office/settings/credit-limit` + `CreditLimitConfigForm` + `GET/PATCH /api/settings/credit-limit`, entry in `SETTINGS_NAV`). **Contacts list** (`/office/customers`) gained a **credit filter** (Any / Manual limit set / No manual limit — the last uses `NOT` so it never collides with the `q`-search `OR`) + a **Credit limit column** (shows the manual ₹, an "Auto" badge when the global toggle is on, else "—"). Seed: `CreditLimitConfig` singleton in `bootstrap-seed` (create-only, auto OFF). New `requirePosVoucher(kind, action)` guard (auth + `canDoPosVoucher` = the Role-page tick or office-equivalent); the open-shift check stays in each route body. "Create means create." Seeds updated (`bootstrap-seed` perm list, `helper-roles` grants). **Also fixed the reported "grant Contacts create/edit not saved" bug**: the Role editor renders a sidebar-link's granular CRUD matrix gated only on the _link_ being in the department pool, but `PUT /api/roles/[id]/permissions` filtered out any id not in the pool — and the pool only carries the `ui:sidebar:*` links, never the granular CRUD, so the ticks were silently dropped. New derived `SIDEBAR_LINK_GRANULAR_IDS` (from the matrix rows) lets the save filter **unlock a link's granular ids once the link itself is pooled** — dept pools _module visibility_, role picks the _granular level_. **DB reconcile (non-destructive):** an idempotent script remapped existing scoped/`manage` grants + pools → flat, then deleted the **13** dead permissions (FK cascade); custom roles preserved (verified 0 deprecated remaining). Two new `InvVoucherType` members: **`ONLINE_INVOICE`** = the fulfilment sale (same economics as `SALE`/`POS` — stock OUT + GST/GL), **`ONLINE_ORDER`** = the storefront order/commitment (OPTIONAL binding — NO GL, NO stock here; reserves stock via the existing order pipeline at CONFIRMED, fulfils into `ONLINE_INVOICE`). New `src/store/posDraftStore.ts` (Zustand, **no `persist` — in-memory singleton** so it survives the `<Link>` route change but not a reload / next shift) holds the **shared slice**: `snapshot` (customer) + `cart` + generic bill adjustments (`shippingAmtStr`, `roundOffStr`, `roundOffManuallyEdited`, `charges`). Setters mirror React's `Dispatch<SetStateAction<T>>`, so every existing call site (`setCart((p)=>…)`, `setCart([])`) is **unchanged** — the swap was declaration-only. Shared types extracted to `src/modules/pos/lib/posDraftTypes.ts` (`Snapshot`/`ProductOption`/`CartLine`/`ChargeRow`/`newChargeRow`), de-duplicated from both screens. **Deliberately kept LOCAL** (not in the store): Sale-only money/shift state (tenders, open shift, surplus) and **server-validated money fields** (coupon, loyalty redemption, gift card) — their ₹ value is tied to the exact cart+customer, so carrying them across a switch risks a stale amount (Phase 2 may revisit once each screen re-validates on cart change). **Edit mode is isolated**: both edit-hydration effects `reset()` on mount + unmount so an existing-voucher edit never leaks into (or out of) the carried draft; the new-sale success path still clears fields individually (keeps the customer for a quick re-bill). Gates: tsc clean, harness GREEN, recompute-stock ZERO DRIFT._ **5D-13 done (the rename): Prisma model `InvVoucher` → `VoucherDetail`** — the 1:1 cosmetic/print sidecar now has its intended name. `@@map("InvVoucher")` keeps the physical table, so it's CODE-ONLY (db already in sync, no data move). Scope was small once the misleading count was understood: the "1000 Prisma.InvVoucher refs" were the GENERATED client (regenerates); real source churn = 47 files of `prisma/tx.invVoucher.` delegate calls + ~9 `Prisma.InvVoucher*` type annotations + 1 root script, plus ~11 schema relation TYPE refs. Done via targeted sed (`prisma|tx|db.invVoucher.`→`.voucherDetail.`, `Prisma.InvVoucher`→`Prisma.VoucherDetail`). **Relation FIELD names + @relation strings KEPT** (`UniversalVoucher.invVoucher`, `Branch.invVouchersAsDest`, `Ticket.invVouchers`, `refVoucher`/`refReturns`, etc.) — field-name ≠ model-name is valid Prisma and avoids churn; the `InvVoucherType` enum is unrelated and untouched. Seeds use the TABLE name in raw TRUNCATEs (preserved) so they're unaffected. Gates: tsc clean, harness GREEN, recompute-stock ZERO DRIFT. **Phase 5 5D is now essentially COMPLETE — the spine owns all primary attributes (type/status/deletedAt/date/branch + binding); the detail is renamed VoucherDetail and holds only frozen/cosmetic print fields + the still-disconnected workflow FKs.** REMAINING (deferred, "rebuild later" per user): workflow FKs (ticket/deal/order/shopOrder, ~120 refs across repairing/deals/parked-Orders-board/ecommerce) → VoucherLink — its own project alongside the Orders-board rebuild; optional cleanups: **GL_POSTING VoucherSeries retired (5D-14, `2d6f1d1b`)**; **`UniversalVoucher.invVoucher`→`voucherDetail` renamed (5D-15, `5e69fdb1`)** — the spine's 1:1 accessor now matches the model; `ShopOrder.invVoucher` left as-is on purpose (mirrors the ecommerce API response key the frontend reads). Still optional: drop the vestigial `sourceJournalEntryId` self-ref (verify lifecycle paths first). **5D-17 done (`eb06b6a0`): `shopOrder` FK DISCONNECTED** (user: a shop order is a customer-portal POS-style order; rebuild its voucher link in the ecommerce module). Dropped `VoucherDetail.shopOrderId`/`shopOrder` + `ShopOrder.invVoucher`; the SHOP_ORDER VoucherLink (dual-written) preserves the data for the rebuild; disconnected the ecommerce read routes (the `.tsx` pages guard on `order.invVoucher?` so they render nothing until rebuilt); removed the inventory `?shopOrderId=` filter (latent untyped-spread bug). Also fixed a 5D-1 latent bug in auditRestore (the `as unknown` cast hid a write to the dropped `journalEntryId`; now links via universalVoucherId). **PRECEDENT for the remaining workflow FKs: disconnect each, rebuild in its own module (don't mix old FK + new VoucherLink).** REMAINING workflow FKs: `ticketId` (~76 refs, repairing), `dealId` (~22, deals/CRM), `orderPipelineId`/`orderStatusId` (Orders board, PARKED) — each to be disconnected when its module is rebuilt. \_Traps: tsc-misses-runtime (excess-property check skips function-returned objects written to InvVoucher); engineGlArm attach-mode RE-WRITES the spine's type/description/voucherNo from the spec._ Remaining Phase 5: finish 5A (Orders-board reader cutover + drop the 5 workflow FK columns — parked behind pipeline work). Verification gap (dev, accepted): voucher/POS/loyalty UI not run-tested.\_
- **2026-06-27 — GL collapsed onto the UniversalVoucher spine; `JournalEntry`/`JournalLine` dropped (Phase 4 G5)**: the long-running "one GL path" refactor reached its endpoint — the `UniversalVoucher` + `VoucherGlLine` spine is now the **sole** general-ledger representation, and the legacy `JournalEntry`/`JournalLine` tables are **dropped** (`db push --accept-data-loss`). `engineGlArm` no longer dual-writes a JE; it creates the spine, self-refs it (`spine.sourceJournalEntryId = spine.id`) and returns `{journalEntryId: spine.id, universalVoucherId: spine.id}`, so `Voucher.journalEntryId` / `InvVoucher.journalEntryId` carry the **spine id** and every id-keyed lifecycle path resolves against the spine with no re-keying. Dropped the dual-write shims (`mirrorJeToSpine`/`syncSpineLifecycle`/`resyncSpineFromJe`); added `setSpineLifecycle`. All lifecycle routes (cancel/delete/restore/post/hard-delete × journal-entries/accountancy-vouchers/inventory-vouchers), both in-place edit paths, `auditRestore`, the journal-entries list/detail, statistics, gl-accounts/ledgers, recompute-balances, seedVoucherSeries, convertParty, and voucherReceiptSample all read/write the spine; balance reversals resolve `normalBalance` via an id→nb map (spine GL lines are scalar/id-only). Seeds updated same commit (§5): `dummy-vouchers` writes the spine directly + self-refs; `hard-clean`/`purge-vouchers` truncate `VoucherGlLine`/`UniversalVoucher`. `Voucher`/`VoucherLine` were **kept** (smaller blast radius — financial-voucher header/lines). Parity harness rewritten to verify the spine directly (retired the dual-write parity tests) — **HARNESS GREEN**, tsc clean, dummy seed runs, cache recomputed to spine truth. Restore tag `pre-g5-drop`. _Verification gap (dev, accepted): the lifecycle routes + journal-entry/financial-voucher UI are not run-tested — smoke-test create→post→cancel for a GL voucher + a journal voucher. Deferred: slim the now-duplicated InvVoucher/Voucher header cols (G4d-2); decide later whether to also drop Voucher/VoucherLine._
- **2026-06-25 — Per-kind GL voucher permissions (matrix-on-role / list-on-dept)**: the accountancy slice of the dept/role redesign. **24 new `finance:voucher:<action>:<kind>` keys** (4 actions × 6 kinds: payment/receipt/contra/credit-note/debit-note/journal) replace the coarse single voucher gate. A new shared, config-driven [`PermissionMatrix`](src/shared/components/PermissionMatrix.tsx) renders a **"GL voucher eligibility" matrix** on the Role editor (rows = kinds, columns = Create/Post/Cancel/Delete), filtered to the role's department pool (locked cells = not offered). The Departments page gets compact **flex voucher-kind toggles** ("what this dept works with"; toggling a kind pools its 4 action-perms) — department decides _what_, role decides _eligibility_. **Action model** (driven by [`voucherMatrix.ts`](src/shared/lib/auth/voucherMatrix.ts)): CANCEL covers cancel + edit + (future) convert-to-draft; **DELETE is the single removal** — soft-delete was already retired (2026-06-09), so the per-kind "hard-delete" was dropped and "delete" is the one removal (generic `finance:voucher:hard-delete` kept only as the Recycle Bin / audit-restore gate). Guards (`requireVoucherPermission`/`userCanVoucher`) check per-kind **OR** the generic key (additive — legacy grants keep working); the GL voucher routes (`/api/accountancy/vouchers/*` + `journal-vouchers` POST) resolve the kind and gate accordingly; `GlVoucherPage` button gates switched to per-kind. Seeds: 24 perms in bootstrap (R1 auto), R5/R6 migrated to per-kind, DEP-FIN pool kind-complete; stale per-kind hard-delete rows pruned. **No accounting math touched** (only guard lines). _Deferred:_ report-access perms (Balance Sheet/P&L/Day Book stay on `accountancy:view:org`), Inventory voucher kinds (reuses the shared matrix), own/branch/org scope enforcement. Docs: [permissions.md](docs/modules/permissions.md).
- **2026-06-24 — Department-wise roles + permission pools (Role Matrix authoring lens)**: roles are now organised **by department** so the Role Matrix stops dumping all ~120 permissions on one screen. Every `Role` carries a required **home department** (`Role.departmentId`) — an **authoring lens, not a data scope** (runtime resolution in `authorizeStaff.ts` is untouched; the pool never participates in permission checks). New `DepartmentPermission` join holds each department's curated **pool**; a role homed there may only be granted permissions from its pool, and the role editor filters the matrix + chips to that pool (verified: Sales Executive shows "25 of 121"). `Department.isAllAccess` (seeded **`DEP-ADMIN`**) means "pool = every permission" (computed live, auto-includes new keys) — `R1` and the legacy `R5` Branch Manager are homed there; its editor shows the full list. **Cross-cutting access** = all-access dept _or_ a multi-position holder (branch manager = one position per department, perms = union, `scope = BRANCH`) — never a null department; `null` `User.role` now means only "created, no position yet". **Pool removal cascades** (strips the permission from roles in that department; the API returns the count, never silent); moving a role to another department strips out-of-pool grants. **Positions derive their department from their role** (`Role.departmentId`) — the position editor shows it read-only; only a role-less seat picks one manually. New: `Role.departmentId` + `Department.isAllAccess` + `DepartmentPermission` (schema), `src/shared/lib/auth/departmentPool.ts`, `src/shared/lib/org/positionDepartment.ts`, `GET`/`PUT /api/departments/[id]/permissions`, a pool picker on `/office/admin/departments`. Seeds: `DEP-ADMIN` + `R1.departmentId` in bootstrap; per-role departments + functional-department pools (union of each dept's roles' perms) in `helper-roles.ts`; `hard-clean` clears `DepartmentPermission`. DB force-reset + reseeded. Docs: [permissions.md](docs/modules/permissions.md) + [seeds.md](docs/seeds.md). _Deferred:_ retire the `R5` mega-role in favour of the multi-position pattern.
- **2026-06-23 — Bootstrap RBAC reduced to `R1` only; starter roles moved to a helper seed**: `bootstrap-seed.ts` now seeds a **single role, `R1` (Admin)** — the only `isSystem: true` role, universal to every trade. The former built-in roles `R2`–`R7` (Service Engineer, Counter/Cashier, Stores & Despatch, Branch Manager, Accountant, Sales Executive) moved verbatim (same grants) into a new trade-specific helper seed **`prisma/seeds/helper-roles.ts`** (`pnpm seed:helper-roles`), created as **non-system / admin-editable** so admins can rename, re-grant, or delete them from the Role Matrix and build whatever the business needs — "all business not use same role". Mirrors the `helper-inventory-masters.ts` pattern. `hard-clean` now wipes every **non-system** role back to the R1-only bootstrap state (unlinks `Position.roleTemplateId` first — restrict FK — then deletes the roles + grants); re-run the helper to restore. `seed:dummy-all` runs `helper-roles` **before** `dummy-users`/`dummy-org-rich` (they assign `R2`/`R3`/`R4` templates). Applied to the live DB non-destructively via reseed + helper (no force-reset). Ecommerce inbox notifications still target `["R1","R2"]` — `R2` is supplied by the helper and a missing role degrades to "no recipients" (no throw). Docs: [permissions.md](docs/modules/permissions.md) + [seeds.md](docs/seeds.md).
- **2026-06-13 — Workflow engine: polymorphic child-entity spawn + `TicketSubStatus → TicketWorker` rename**: a **Worker** (the renamed sub-status) reaching a `spawnPipelineId` now creates a child in **whatever entity rides the target pipeline** — Ticket (P1/P2), Task (P4), or Deal (P3/P5/P6) — dispatched through a data-driven `CHILD_ENTITIES` registry ([src/modules/repairing/lib/childEntities.ts](src/modules/repairing/lib/childEntities.ts)) keyed by a new `Pipeline.entityType`, **not** a hardcoded `ticket.create`. Unsupported targets (voucher P7 / delivery P8 / refund P9 — protected accounting / order-bound) **log + no-op** rather than fabricate a corrupt row. Uniform back-link (`parentTicketId`/`parentWorkerId`/`context`) on Task & Deal lets the parent **mirror children of any type** across tables (`listAllChildren`); ticket children report back via `parentWorkerId` + `parentSignal`. Then the **whole `TicketSubStatus` model → `TicketWorker`** (delegate `prisma.ticketWorker`, parallel to `TicketStatus`; `SubStatusTransition → WorkerTransition`; FKs `subStatusId→workerId`, `successWorkerId`, `failWorkerId`, `parentWorkerId`, `startWorkerId`, `addedBy/removedByWorkerId`; routes `/api/workers` + `/api/tickets/[id]/worker`; settings `/workflow/workers` "Workers" next to "Works"; permission key `tickets:substatus:set` kept stable). Full production build green; DB force-reset + reseeded under the new names. Frames the **🧭 Design: Workflow engine — the Worker model** section below. _Pending:_ the Action registry (Work `actionType`) — first slice = **Dial** on W1.
- **2026-06-09 — Payment Methods settings (gateway catalog for counter + online)**: new **Settings → Payment Methods** page (`/office/settings/payment-methods`) — the global catalog of payment gateways/methods that the heavy gateway config lives in (kept off the per-counter screen). New `PaymentGateway` model in `prisma/schema/pos.prisma` (provider, displayName, environment TEST/LIVE, `enabledForCounter`/`enabledForOnline`, non-secret `configJson`, sortOrder, isActive) + CRUD at `/api/settings/payment-methods[/[id]]`; the create/update API enforces **≤1 online gateway per environment**. The provider list is an **extensible code registry** (`src/modules/pos/lib/paymentGateways.ts` — Razorpay, Cashfree, generic UPI/manual now; PayU/PayPal are one-line additions, no migration). **Secrets stay in env vars** (owner's choice): the page never accepts or returns key/secret values — it only reports whether each provider's env vars are present (✓/✗) via `credentialStatus.ts` + `/api/settings/payment-methods/credential-status`. A counter **picks** a catalog entry for its `GATEWAY` rail (additive nullable `PosCounterPaymentAccount.gatewayId`, `onDelete: SetNull`, dropdown in `/office/admin/counters`) — so counter 1 = Razorpay, counter 2 = PayU, etc.; the **POS sale path (`/api/pos/sale`, `InvVoucherTender`, GL) is unchanged** (gatewayId is metadata). Online checkout reads the active online gateway (`getActiveOnlineGateway` → `/api/shop/payment-config`, non-secret name only) and shows "online payment via &lt;name&gt; coming soon" — **config-only; the live Razorpay charge + webhook stay parked**. Bootstrap seeds no gateways (operator config); `hard-clean` DELETEs `payment_gateways` (not TRUNCATE CASCADE) so counter rails survive unlinked. Gated by `settings:manage`. Additive schema, plain `db push`. _Deferred:_ live online charge wiring (signature-verified webhook → order paid). See [docs/modules/settings.md](docs/modules/settings.md) + [docs/modules/pos.md](docs/modules/pos.md).
- **2026-06-07 — O2C Phase 1b: POS Estimate + Order as full POS clones + 2 new voucher types**: reshaped Phase 1 per owner — all three POS modes now share the **exact POS billing screen** (customer panel + discounts), differing by **colour** + save/print. `PosScreen` was **cloned** into `PosEstimateScreen` (amber) + `PosOrderScreen` (indigo) — protected Sale screen untouched (clones are separate files; DRY pass deferred). Each clone's save posts to its own endpoint instead of `/api/pos/sale` (no payment/shift): **`POST /api/pos/estimate` → `POS_ESTIMATE`**, **`POST /api/pos/order` → `POS_ORDER`** (two **new non-posting `InvVoucherType`s** — NO GL, NO stock; wired through the enum, `PREFIX` numbering `POSEV-`/`POSOD-`, allowed-types, print templates, register badges, and a defensive `isCommitment` guard; the GL/stock guards are _negative_ so new types auto-skip). Both reuse `createPosQuoteVoucher` (shared lib) which carries the **tier/manual bill discount** + tag-price snapshots and captures a CRM Deal (`findOpenDealForParty`). **Estimate price-privacy:** cashier screen shows discounts (to negotiate); the **customer copy prints the tag/list price, discounts hidden** — governed by a new **Org Defaults setting** (`OrgDefaults.posEstimateShowDiscountsJson`, Settings → Org Defaults, default all-hidden) read by the receipt builder (`buildInvSample` swaps to tag price for `POS_ESTIMATE` unless a discount is toggled on). Order prints the real price. Schema additive (2 enum values + 1 JSON field, plain `db push`; PREFIX fallback so no seed needed). _Loyalty/coupon don't bind on a non-committal quote (deferred); stock reservation + Order→pipeline are Phase 2/3; cosmetic trim of clone POS-only UI (shift/return/exp) deferred._ Supersedes the slim Phase-1 estimate screen.
- **2026-06-07 — O2C Phase 1: POS Estimate mode (price-privacy quote → captures a Deal)**: POS is now **mode-first** — a `PosModeSwitch` (Sale / Order / Estimate, colour-coded) on the party bar of both the existing POS screen (additive, sale logic untouched) and a new slim **Estimate page** at `/vouchers/pos/estimate` (its own full-screen shell, separate from the frozen POS Sale voucher). Estimate mode prints the **barcode/tag (cap) price with all discounts stripped** (price privacy, verbal negotiation) — verified end-to-end: the printed `ESTIMATE / QUOTATION` shows the tag price (₹273), never the strategy price. New isolated `POST /api/pos/estimate` (gated by `accountancy:view:org`, same as POS sale — no `deals:manage` needed) creates an **`ESTIMATE` InvVoucher** (no stock, no GL — reuses the existing voucher type + `invNextRef`/`calcLineTotals`) and, when a party is attached, **captures a CRM Deal** (find-or-create the party's open deal via new `findOpenDealForParty` dedup, stamp a SYSTEM activity, soft-link `InvVoucher.dealId`). Anonymous estimate = quote only, no deal. Schema is additive (`InvVoucher.dealId` ↔ `Deal.vouchers`, plain `db push`; seeds unaffected — neither bootstrap nor dummy create InvVouchers). Also passed the tx through `{ timeout: 30_000, maxWait: 10_000 }` after hitting P2028 on the pooler (see [[interactive_tx_timeout_p2028]] pattern). First slice of the [O2C redesign](docs/srs/order-to-cash.md). _Order mode button is rendered disabled ("coming soon") — Phase 3._
- **2026-06-06 — Ecommerce COD MVP (rebuild of the legacy shop order flow)**: online orders are now **Pay-on-Delivery** and ride the seeded **`sales-orders` pipeline (P7)** as their single source of truth. `POST /api/shop/orders` creates a `ShopOrder` with the new `paymentMethod` (`ShopPaymentMethod` enum, default `COD`, `ONLINE` parked) and drops it on the pipeline entry stage — checkout no longer calls Razorpay (`/shop/checkout` → "Place COD order"). Pipeline transitions (`transitionOrderPipelineStatus`) are now **pure status moves**: the old auto path — a DRAFT SALE voucher (`createOrderInvVoucher`) on "Confirmed" plus a **crude hardcoded JE** (`postSaleJournal`, codes `1010`/`4150`, no GST/AR) on "Paid" — was **removed**, killing the double-book hazard and the bypassed stock ledger; **invoice + delivery + GL + stock are all manual** via the office SALE voucher (which posts stock via `recordStockMovement` + GL with real GST via `postInvVoucherToGl`). `transitionShopOrderStatus` deleted; the Razorpay webhook is parked (records a payment id only, never transitions — `TODO(payment-gateway)`). **Shop now shows the barcode tag price** (`invProductToPublic(p, bands)` → `barcodeTagPaise`, fallback effective) so shelf == shop == receipt; order lines snapshot that price. New **shop-qualification gate** (`shopQualification` + `resolveShopListing` + `slugifyProduct`): a product lists only with image+description+price; the ecommerce product form shows a readiness checklist, gates the "Listed in shop" switch, and auto-lists/auto-slugs qualified products; public shop APIs re-check defensively. Schema is additive (`ShopOrder.paymentMethod` + `ShopPaymentMethod`, plain `db push`). New helpers: `src/shared/lib/shop/barcodeBands.ts`, `src/modules/ecommerce/lib/{shopQualification,shopListing}.ts`. The publish gate is **owner-configurable** (`OrgDefaults.shopPublishRulesJson` + `GET/PUT /api/ecommerce/publish-rules` + **Settings → E-Commerce** `/office/settings/ecommerce`): image/description/price/shop-category/MRP/in-stock each toggleable, default = image+description+price (edited in **Settings → E-Commerce** `/office/settings/ecommerce`; the settings page retries on load and disables Save until the real state loads so a failed GET can't mask/overwrite saved rules). An **out-of-stock policy** was added (org-wide `OrgDefaults.shopOutOfStockPolicy` HIDE/NOTIFY/BACKORDER, default NOTIFY, + per-product `InvProduct.allowBackorder` override): `resolveAvailability` drives storefront visibility + `stockStatus`/`orderable` on the public DTO, with out-of-stock badges / disabled cart / backorder labels and order-intake backorder support (`/api/ecommerce/stock-policy`). The Products admin was also reworked (All/Published/Pending tabs, Brand/Group columns, Category/Brand/Group/Status/Stock/Active filters, expandable rows, sticky scroll, breadcrumb + Esc) and the module promoted out of the `(to-do)` route group with sidebar nav enabled. **Orders list redesigned** (pipeline-status badges, status tabs, channel/COD, search, paging) and a **pipeline-transition bug fixed** (order detail read `toTransitions`/incoming instead of `fromTransitions`/outgoing, so the entry stage showed "No further actions"). **Redundant surfaces removed**: the ecommerce **Coupons** + **Categories** tabs/pages/APIs and the legacy `ShopCoupon` model are gone — promotions live in Customer Retention (unified `Coupon`), categories in Inventory masters; E-Commerce is now **Dashboard · Orders · Products** (sidebar updated to match). See [docs/modules/sales.md](docs/modules/sales.md). _Deferred:_ payment gateways, stock reservation, auto-invoice, server-side cart/address book.
- **2026-06-05 — Deals pipeline + CRM-grade Contacts list**: (a) the HubSpot-style deal pipeline (kanban board + activity timeline, position-owned, JSON-actor) was **renamed CRM → Deals** — model `CrmLead`/`CrmLeadActivity` → `Deal`/`DealActivity`, permissions `crm:* → deals:*`, routes `/office/crm/deals` + `/api/deals`, module `src/modules/deals`, seeded **P6** pipeline relabelled `name:"deals"`/`label:"Deals"`. The actor-snapshot helper lives in `src/shared/lib/auth/actorSnapshot.ts` (shared by Deals + the work board; `taskActor.ts` delegates). New [docs/modules/deals.md](docs/modules/deals.md). (b) **Customers relabelled "Contacts"** and given big-list tooling: `Person`/`Company` gained `lifecycleStage` (`ContactLifecycle` LEAD/PROSPECT/CUSTOMER/INACTIVE), `ownerPositionId` (seat owner, holder resolved live), and `tags String[]`. New paginated/filterable `GET /api/contacts` (People; q/lifecycle/owner/tag/hasDeal/sort via `parsePagination`) + `POST /api/contacts/bulk` (set lifecycle / assign owner / add·remove tag / activate·deactivate). New `ContactsWorkspace` at `/office/customers` (paged table via `PaginationBar`, filter bar, multi-select **bulk bar**, **CSV export** with BOM). `dummy-customers.ts` now seeds 250 synthetic contacts with a lifecycle/tag spread so the list demonstrates scale. **Deals stay linked to a contact** via `personId`/`companyId` (a contact's deals are queryable via `/api/deals?personId=`). Won links a customer; it does not auto-create a sale (protected sales flow untouched). _Deferred:_ a Deals tab on the contact detail pages and import field-map extension (lifecycle/owner/tags on CSV import). New [docs/modules/contacts.md](docs/modules/contacts.md).
- **2026-06-05 — Administration work board (pipeline-based internal todos, position-owned)**: _(superseded — the `Task`/`TaskComment` models described below were absorbed into the unified `Ticket` by the Unified Ticket merge; a task is now a bare `Ticket` on P4 with no detail table, and comments are `TicketLog` rows. `prisma/schema/task.prisma` and `docs/modules/administration.md` no longer exist.)_ new device-free `Task` + `TaskComment` models (`prisma/schema/task.prisma`) that ride the shared `Pipeline`/`TicketStatus` tables on the seeded **P4 "internal-management"** pipeline (Open → In Review → In Progress → On Hold → Done) — repair `Ticket` was unusable (required `deviceId`, warranty/billing baggage). **Ownership is a Position (seat), not a user**: `Task.assignedPositionId` → `Position`, and the effective owner is resolved live from `UserPosition.holders`, so open work transfers automatically when staff resign or change branch (matches the position-based RBAC). **No User FKs** — the actor of an action is a frozen JSON snapshot (`Task.createdBy` / `TaskComment.author`, shape `TaskActor`, built by `buildTaskActor`), so audit history survives staff turnover. Kanban **Work Board** at `/office/administration` (native HTML5 drag-and-drop with priority left-accent cards showing position + current holder; optimistic stage moves; "My tasks" = held-position filter; create/edit dialogs with an assign-to-position picker; per-task activity thread mixing user `COMMENT`s and auto `SYSTEM` change-logs; terminal stage stamps `completedAt`). Server layout adds a `PathBreadcrumb` + Esc-back; the sidebar section carries a button-bar **"New Task +"** deep-link (`?new=1`). `/api/admin-tasks` CRUD + `/[id]/comments`; new `administration:tasks:view|manage` permissions (R1 auto, R5 granted) gating the API, page layout, and sidebar section. `dummy-tasks.ts` seed (6 demo tasks on positions, in `seed:dummy-all`); `Task`/`TaskComment` added to `hard-clean` `TRANSACTION_TABLES`. DRY by design — other modules can adopt `Task` later. See [docs/modules/administration.md](docs/modules/administration.md).
- **2026-06-04 — Per-product additive point (`PRODUCT` strategy dimension)**: the additive-margin engine gained a per-SKU override. New `PRODUCT` value on the `StrategyPointDimension` enum (keyed by product id); `margin% = base + brand + category + group + product`. It is _added_ to the master sum (negative discounts a poor performer even when its brand/category/group all add positive points; positive lifts a star SKU under weak masters), so unlike `marginPercent`/manual-price it keeps the SKU following future brand/category/group changes. Edited inline via a new **`Product pts`** column on the `/office/pricing-strategy/strategies` preview table, which is now paged by a new reusable **`PaginationBar`** (`src/shared/components/PaginationBar.tsx`) replacing the old silent 300-row cap. Engine (`additiveMargin.ts`), `strategyPointsSaveSchema`, and the `points` GET/PUT route all carry the `product` map; the `items`/`apply`/`recomputePrices` callers needed no change. No seed change. See [docs/modules/pricing-strategy.md](docs/modules/pricing-strategy.md).
- **2026-06-03 — Additional-cost (shipping/courier) allocation layer**: a decision-support layer that attributes already-booked courier expense to products as a per-unit cost for **pricing & margin only — it never posts to the GL** (accountancy stays untouched, the owner's hard constraint). Nominate an `AccountGroup` as the pool (`OrgDefaults.shippingPoolGroupId`); every POSTED debit to a ledger under it is a waiting "entry". New `/office/pricing-strategy/costing` page: per entry, **Allocate** the product-freight share — voucher-driven (`AllocateVoucherDialog` + `costing/draws/batch`): pick the PURCHASE/GRN voucher and spread freight across its product lines via per-unit inputs (by-value / by-qty auto-distribute) — and/or **Dismiss** the office-courier remainder so it leaves the waiting list (one courier bill often covers both). Draw history is inline-editable. New `AdditionalCostAllocation` model (`additional_cost_allocations`, kind `ALLOCATION|DISMISSAL`, rows against a `JournalLine` debit + `sourceInvVoucherId`) + `AdditionalCostAllocationKind` enum + `costPool.ts` (LAST-allocation derive rule) + `costing/pool` & `costing/draws`(+`[id]` DELETE) APIs. `items` API now exposes per-unit `additionalCostPaise` (separate from the pristine `resolvedCostPaise`); `additiveMargin.ts` `additivePriceWithSurcharge` folds it in **before** GST; Items + Update & Print Labels reflect it in Computed/Profit. Pool-group selector on the Org Defaults settings form. Dummy demo in `dummy-vouchers.ts`. **Sale/POS margin display deferred** (protected surface). See [docs/modules/pricing-strategy.md](docs/modules/pricing-strategy.md).
- **2026-06-03 — Per-customer credit limit edit UI**: `Account.creditLimit` is no longer dormant — `PATCH /api/accounts/[id]` now writes it (gated by `customers:edit:org` for a person ledger, `companies:edit:org` for a company), and a new `AccountCreditLimitCard` ([src/modules/customer/components](src/modules/customer/components/AccountCreditLimitCard.tsx)) sits on both the Person and Company **Profile** tabs. `GET /api/person/[id]` and `GET /api/companies/[id]` now pass `creditLimit` through. Deny-by-default semantics preserved (blank/0 = cash-only, positive = credit headroom); enforcement at sale time via `checkCreditLimit` is unchanged. See [docs/modules/people.md](docs/modules/people.md).
- **2026-06-01 — Loyalty v1 (POS earn + redeem wallet)**: new `loyalty.prisma` (config singleton + per category/group/product earn-rule multipliers + per-customer account + lots + audit ledger), `src/modules/loyalty/{lib,components}`, and five `/api/loyalty/*` routes. Identified customers earn points on POS sales (rate resolves product→group→category→global) and redeem them as a bill discount. **Expiry is dynamic + lazy** (no cron): a lot is valid iff `earnedAt > now − expiryDays`, computed live — raising the window revives recently-lapsed points, lowering it drops them; balance is never cached. Config matches+beats Vyapar's reward screen (min-invoice-to-earn, min-invoice-to-redeem, expiry on/off in days, min-points). **Earning is GL-silent**; **redemption** posts to a seeded **"Loyalty Points Redeemed"** expense ledger via a negative `InvVoucherCharge` (the protected `postInvVoucherToGl` math is reused unchanged); outstanding points are shown as a read-only **memo liability** (not booked). Admin hub at `/office/customer-retention/loyalty` + a Loyalty tab on the customer profile. New permission `loyalty:manage`. Also **fixed a latent POS bug**: the tier discount was computed client-side but never sent, so any tier > 0% failed the server payment-total check — `/api/pos/sale` now accepts `discountAmt` (→ Discount Allowed). v1 is POS-only; low-margin protection is manual (set loss-makers to 0×). See [docs/modules/loyalty.md](docs/modules/loyalty.md).
- **2026-05-21 — GL engine reference split**: every `JournalEntry` auto-created by the posting engine (Sale, Purchase, POS, Receipt, Payment, Contra, user JV, returns, shift variance, e-commerce JE, reversal JE) now uses a new `GL_POSTING` VoucherSeries with reference prefix `GL-…` (was `JE-…`, which visually collided with the user's Journal Voucher). The user Journal Voucher series locks to `JV-…`. `GET /api/accountancy/journal-entries` hides engine output by default (`?includeEngine=1` to inspect); `POST /api/accountancy/journal-entries` is deprecated and returns 410 — all user journals go through `/api/accountancy/journal-vouchers`. Day Book duplicates (one row per sale + a second "JOURNAL" row) are gone. Voucher/InvVoucher table split kept intentionally — see [accountancy.md](docs/modules/accountancy.md) "Two engines" + "Why two voucher tables" sections.
- **2026-05-20 — Admin + Settings V1.5 (in-section nav)**: `office/admin/layout.tsx` + `AdminNav` and `office/settings/layout.tsx` + `SettingsNav` — permission-filtered grouped tab bars (same pattern as voucher registers), sourced from `ADMIN_NAV` / `SETTINGS_NAV` in `constants.ts`. Admin `loading.tsx` skeleton keeps nav visible while page content loads.
- **2026-05-20 — Settings module extracted from Admin**: 7 pages (pipelines, statuses, workers, org-defaults, year-ending, naming-convention, printer-settings) + `OrgDefaultsForm` moved to `/office/settings/*` and `src/modules/settings/`. New [docs/modules/settings.md](docs/modules/settings.md). Hard cut, no compat redirects from old `/office/admin/<page>` URLs. `next.config.ts` cleaned up to avoid hijacking the new `/office/settings/*` paths.

## 📋 Next up (prioritised)

- **HR & People — all three phases shipped** (staff record → payroll → attendance / holidays / leave, then pay rules, My Info and the printable salary slip, 2026-09-20 → 22). Nothing agreed is left open; the next HR ask opens a new slot. Design: `🧭 Design: HR & People` below.
0. **Order-to-Cash (O2C) redesign — agreed 2026-06-07; Phase 1 SHIPPED.** Full design in [docs/srs/order-to-cash.md](docs/srs/order-to-cash.md), summary in the **🧭 Design: Order-to-Cash** section below. Headline: one **Order backbone** fed by all channels (counter/phone/online/B2B as adapters); **counter Sale = always hand-to-hand**, deferred cases are Orders identical to phone orders; **POS mode-first** (Sale/Order/Estimate on separate pages, frozen POS untouched); **parent Order pipeline + child pipelines** (Delivery/Recovery/Return); **lazy GL ledgers** (account on first transaction); **3-tier lead funnel** (RawLead→Contact→Customer); pluggable **carrier abstraction** (Shiprocket/own-boy/pickup) with rate-shopping. Accountancy engine reused unchanged. **Phase 1 (POS Estimate mode) shipped 2026-06-07** — see Recently shipped. **Next phases:** 2 = stock **reservation** layer (on-hand/reserved/available, per branch); 3 = extract **Order** out of Ecommerce into its own backbone module + POS **Order mode**; 4 = lazy ledger + lead→customer promotion; 5 = **Delivery module + Rate Engine** (own-boy/manual first); 6 = Recovery / Return&Refund pipelines.
1. **Deal pipeline redesign — match HubSpot stages + document-per-stage + win-probability (agreed 2026-06-08, build pending).** Reshape the Deals pipeline (P6) to MATCH HubSpot's default sales pipeline so the funnel + forecasting mirror the reference CRM:
   `Appointment Scheduled (20%) → Qualified To Buy (40%) → Presentation Scheduled (60%) → Decision Maker Bought-In (80%) → Contract Sent (90%) → Closed Won (100%)` · `Closed Lost (0%)`.
   - **Win-probability % per stage** → weighted-pipeline forecast on the board (deal value × stage %), like HubSpot's "Weighted amount".
   - **Document mapped to stages**, with a **Create [doc]** action on the deal card that **auto-advances** the deal to that stage:
     - Presentation Scheduled → **Estimate / Quotation** (`POS_ESTIMATE`)
     - Contract Sent → **Sales Order** (`POS_ORDER`) **+ Proforma Invoice** (a NEW voucher type — invoice-shaped doc to collect an advance; has its own validity/payment-terms, never a GST/tax invoice)
     - Closed Won → **Tax Invoice** (the Sale)
   - Schema: add `winProbability Int?` + `documentKind String?` to `TicketStatus`. Keep existing stage IDs **migration-safe** (relabel, don't renumber) so current deals don't break; add new IDs only for genuinely new stages.
   - Deal stage ≠ documents (HubSpot keeps Quotes a separate object too) — documents link to the deal via `dealId` / `deal.vouchers`, they aren't the stages themselves.
   - Later: a 2nd **short retail pipeline** (Lead → Quoted → Won/Lost) for walk-ins, since they don't traverse the full B2B funnel.
2. **Customer display: show the product being scanned — photo + price + discount (asked 2026-09-04).** The DQ11 currently shows the cart list, the QR and the idle slideshow. Add a per-line screen: as each item is scanned, render its **product photo, price, and the discount given** so the customer sees what they are being charged for, item by item. Notes for whoever builds it: the display is driven from `hardware-bridge/qr.js` (`pos/customer`, `pos/cart`, rendered with sharp and pushed over serial), so this is a new screen + a new command there plus a call from the POS cart; product photos will need resizing to the DQ11's panel size and a fallback for items with no photo; decide how long the item screen holds before falling back to the cart/idle view. Idle slideshow folder is now `Pictures\shop-ads`.
3. **Doc consistency sweep** — keep this file as the only source of status; resist re-adding "shipped" markers to module docs.
4. **✅ DONE 2026-09-27 — `/lab` decision:** deleted.
5. **Universal Customer Finder** — build the multi-signal Front Office search: narrow to a customer by **any** clue — name / phone / email / address (✅ done) + product **bought / repaired / inquired / ordered online** + **month / last-active**. Move from client-side merge to one server-side `/api/customers/find` endpoint. Phased; next concrete step is **Phase B — products bought**. Full design, signal inventory, endpoint sketch, and gotchas in the **🧭 Design: Universal Customer Finder** section below.
6. **✅ DONE 2026-08-01 — Voucher lifecycle: recycle-bin restore fixed, soft-delete removed (agreed 2026-06-08).** Lifecycle cleanup discovered/decided while fixing hard-delete & cancel:
   - **Recycle-bin restore was silently broken** for inventory vouchers: `auditRestore.ts` still wrote the pre-Phase-5C shape (nested `lines` on `VoucherDetail`, `voucherId` on `InvStockLedger`), hidden from `tsc` by `as unknown as` casts, so no hard-deleted inventory voucher could actually be restored. Fixed to re-create goods lines + stock rows against the recreated spine.
   - **There is only ONE delete.** The soft-delete tier is gone: routes `/api/inventory/vouchers/[id]/{delete,restore}`, `/api/accountancy/vouchers/[id]/{delete,restore}` and `/api/accountancy/journal-entries/[id]/{delete,restore}` deleted; `deleteFields`/`restoreFields`/`ensureDeletable`/`ensureRestorable` dropped from `voucherLifecycle.ts`; the Day Book menu's Restore + Hard-Delete branch and the `Deleted` status filter (registers, `useRegisterFilters`, `useCombinedVouchers`, `includeDeleted` on the 3 list APIs) removed. Verified first: **0 of 110 spine rows had `deletedAt` set**, and nothing wrote it. `useRegisterFilters` now strips a stale `"DELETED"` out of localStorage on hydrate so nobody is left with a permanently empty register. **Kept deliberately:** the `deletedAt` columns + the `deletedAt: null` read filters (now always-true guards) — dropping them needs a schema push and a sweep through Balance Sheet / valuation.
   - **Recycle bin re-gated on the DELETE key (2026-08-01).** `GET /api/audit` and `POST /api/audit/[id]/restore` demanded `admin:bypass`, which the Admin role does NOT hold — so the bin listed **nothing** for every user and Restore 403'd, while 43 recoverable snapshots sat invisible. Both now use `userCanRestoreAudit` (`src/shared/lib/auth/permissions.ts`): **whoever may delete a voucher kind may restore it**, via the same per-kind `delete` key the hard-delete routes check (`subtype` → kind; JOURNAL_ENTRY → `JOURNAL`). The list filters per row after the fetch, since the key depends on each row's subtype. No permission-catalog change, so no seed updates needed. Verified live: list 403→200 with 43 rows, and a full create → post → hard-delete → restore → hard-delete round-trip returned stock and GL to baseline exactly.
   - **Permanent delete added (2026-08-01), super-admin only.** `DELETE /api/audit/[id]` destroys one recycle-bin snapshot — the last surviving copy of that voucher — gated on `admin:bypass`, i.e. the env super admin (`SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL`/`SUPER_ADMIN_PASSWORD`), which is a code-only sentinel that is never seeded and un-grantable in the Role Matrix. Deliberately a higher bar than restore: putting a voucher back is recoverable, erasing it is not. The Recycle Bin shows a trash button (two-step arm → "Delete forever") only to holders of `admin:bypass`; normal staff get 403 from the API regardless.
   - **✅ Un-cancel (Restore) added 2026-09-13 — a CANCELLED voucher can be put back to POSTED.** Asked for after the owner noticed the Recycle Bin recovers deleted vouchers but nothing recovered cancelled ones. `POST /api/{inventory/vouchers,accountancy/vouchers,accountancy/journal-entries}/[id]/restore`, each gated by **the same permission as its cancel** — whoever may cancel a voucher kind may restore it, so no permission-catalog change and no seed run. Restore is the **mirror of cancel, not a fresh posting**: every artifact cancel writes is identifiable and keyed to the voucher's own spine, so restore deletes those rows and puts the counters back (`restoreVoucherInTx.ts`, `deleteCancelCounterRows` in `stockMovement.ts`, `undoGiftCardRefundForVoucher`, `restoreCouponRedemption`, `restoreLoyaltyAfterCancel`). That covers stock, GL, gift-card money, the coupon usage slot and loyalty points. Three things worth knowing: (1) **stock counter-rows are split by id, not by `amount < 0`** — a free-issue line reverses to `amount` 0, which no sign test can tell from its own original, and the halves are checked to mirror each other before any delete, throwing rather than guessing; (2) the restore **AuditLog row is written with `restoredAt` already set**, because the Day Book treats every `restoredAt: null` audit row as a deletion tombstone — left null, un-cancelling would have made the voucher vanish from the day book; (3) when the rewards the cancel handed back have since been spent on another bill, the route answers **409 + a `conflicts` list** instead of acting, and the dialog shows those in plain words with **Restore anyway / Cancel restore** (`findRestoreConflicts` in `restoreConflicts.ts`). UI is the shared strip ([LifecycleActions.tsx](src/modules/accountancy/components/LifecycleActions.tsx)), so it appears on every voucher detail page at once. **Not** added to the 3-dot row menu (`VoucherActionsMenu`), which does not receive the lifecycle status.
   - **Stock reversal is now mirror-based.** Cancel / hard-delete / restore reverse or re-create the ledger rows the voucher actually wrote and finish with `recomputeProductStock`, replacing three hand-copied type lists that had drifted (cancel never reversed `SALE_RETURN` — every POS return — nor `PURCHASE_RETURN` / rejections / `ONLINE_INVOICE`, and dropped `freeQty`). Direction has one source: `stockDirectionForType` in [stockMovement.ts](src/modules/inventory/lib/stockMovement.ts).
   - **Single `AuditLog` table** — collapse the 3 structurally-identical audit tables (`InvVoucherAudit`, `VoucherAudit`, `JournalEntryAudit`) into one with an `entityType` discriminator (`INV_VOUCHER | GL_VOUCHER | JOURNAL_ENTRY`) + a nullable `restoredAt`. They were never coupled (`originalId` isn't a FK), and one hard-delete already writes to two of them (voucher + its JE). Repoint the 3 hard-delete writers; drop the old tables. **Schema change → same-commit updates to `bootstrap-seed.ts` + `hard-clean.ts`** (the audit tables are in its wipe list).
   - **Universal coverage** — EVERY voucher delete is audited regardless of type/state: GL/stock vouchers, GL-silent docs (ESTIMATE/POS_ESTIMATE/orders), GL vouchers (Payment/Receipt/etc.), journal entries, DRAFT and POSTED alike. Nothing exempt — anything removed is recoverable.
   - **Recycle-bin + restore-from-audit UI** — a view over `AuditLog` (the JSON snapshots are the recycle bin). Restore = a small **re-import + re-post** pipeline: re-create voucher rows from JSON (old ids gone, `reference` preserved/unique), **stitch the voucher snapshot to its JE snapshot** (matched by `originalRef`), re-create the `JournalEntry`+lines, re-apply cached `Account.balance`/`stock` + `InvStockLedger`. Caveat: inbound links (shopOrder/ticket/deal/additional-cost) were `SetNull`/cascade on delete and won't auto-reconnect.
   - **~~Then drop soft-delete + Restore~~ — ✅ done 2026-08-01** (see the summary at the top of this item). The soft-delete tier (`deletedAt` on voucher/JE, the `/delete` + `/restore` routes, the Restore UI) was redundant with **Cancel** (a clean single-reversal, mark-CANCELLED, stays visible). Executed as decided: routes + UI removed, `deletedAt` columns and the `deletedAt: null` filters left inert (always-true) to avoid a risky sweep through Balance Sheet / valuation. Remaining from this bullet: keep `delete` only for **drafts** (zero impact) — and **route draft delete through the audited hard-delete path so drafts are ALSO recoverable from the recycle bin** (every removal is auditable/restorable). Restore = **faithful snapshot restore** (re-insert exactly, keep original reference). **Also raise convert-to-draft permission** ([status/route.ts](src/app/api/inventory/vouchers/[id]/status/route.ts)) from `ACCOUNTANCY_VIEW_ORG` (view-only!) → `INVENTORY_VOUCHER_CANCEL` (same gravity as Cancel — both un-finalize a document). **Sequence: build recycle-bin restore FIRST, drop soft-delete LAST.** Full design in `~/.claude/plans/cozy-waddling-bee.md`.
7. **✅ DONE 2026-08-18 — Day Book: paginated, server-filtered, counter-cached.**
   - **Was:** three endpoints merged in the browser, defaulting to the whole FY and pulling every voucher in it on every visit. Since Phase 5 all three read the SAME table (`UniversalVoucher`), so the "merge" was querying one table three times — and **double-listed every journal** (`FINANCIAL_TYPES` in `/api/accountancy/vouchers` includes `JOURNAL`, and `/api/accountancy/journal-entries` returns it again), showing each journal as two rows (`gl-N` + `je-N`).
   - **Now:** one endpoint `GET /api/accountancy/reports/day-book` — single spine query, cursor `(date desc, id desc)`, **50 rows per fetch**, infinite scroll. A period is a boundary, not a download: picking "This FY" loads 50 rows and stops. Day Book opens on **Today** (`useRegisterFilters(key, defaultPreset)`; other registers keep `thisFY`). `useCombinedVouchers.ts` → `useDayBook.ts`.
   - **Filtering is server-side** (kind / status / branch / category / search) — with paging, filtering in the browser would only filter the rows already pulled. Kind groupings + Optional/Inventory sets moved to `src/modules/accountancy/lib/voucherKinds.ts` so page and route cannot drift (they already had once: POS estimate/order sat in neither set).
   - **Normalised response** — rows carry ids; branch / user / party names live once in `refs`. A rename therefore fixes every cached row by changing one map entry, which is what let the **24h cache-discard be removed entirely**: it existed only because a renamed branch/user/party was invisible to a row-level delta.
   - **Cache is counter-only.** Rows persist to IndexedDB only when the counter bridge answers on `localhost:9191` (`src/shared/lib/bridge` + `useBridgePresent`); on any other machine nothing is written and any copy from a previous life is purged. The bridge is on localhost, so a counter still detects itself with the internet down and stays readable offline.
   - **Delta sync** via `?since` — changed rows plus `AuditLog` tombstones (deletion is physical here, so a changed-row query can never see it), with an overflow escape hatch that tells the client to re-page from the top.
   - **Schema:** `updatedAt` added to `Branch`, `User`, `Account` (+ `@@index([updatedAt])` on Account) so name changes can be _synced_ rather than expired. **Requires `pnpm prisma db push`.**
   - **Unpaid only (2026-09-18).** A tick beside Category keeps only bills that still owe something (`DUE` / `PARTIAL`). Settlement is not a stored column — it is movement-minus-linked, worked out per page — so `?settlement=open` cannot be a `where` clause: the route pre-filters in SQL to what could possibly carry a bill (has a party, POSTED, not a documentary kind) and then **scans forward in chunks**, measuring each chunk and keeping the open ones, until a page of 50 is filled or the chunk cap is hit. The cursor is always an id actually scanned, so the walk stays gap-free; a capped request returns a short page with a cursor and the normal scroll continues it. In delta mode a bill that has just been paid off is reported as a **removal**, since omitting it would leave it on the client's screen. Cost: ~3.5s for a recent page against live, more the deeper the scroll, versus ~1s unfiltered.
   - **Known gap:** the "Posted" figure now covers loaded rows only and is labelled `Posted (loaded)`. A true period total needs an aggregate that the derived `totalAmount` (detail total _or_ Σ debit legs) does not allow cheaply — revisit if it's wanted.
   - **TODO (raised 2026-08-19) — restrict non-admin login to counter machines.** Considered widening the IndexedDB cache to every browser (not just counter-bridge machines) to cut DB reads further; rejected — business data must not persist on a random/shared machine. Counter-only stays as the permanent rule, not a temporary one. Once non-admin logins are locked to counter (bridge-present) machines, "has the bridge" and "is a trusted machine" become the same fact, which is what makes counter-only caching safe long-term. Until then, don't cache anywhere the bridge isn't present.
8. **DRY: structural shared-helper extractions (deferred from the 2026-06-10 DRY pass).** The mechanical sweep already landed 6 shared helpers + migrated ~100 files: `extractApiError` (`src/shared/lib/api`), `parseDecimalInput`/`parseDigitsInput`/`parseSignedInput` + `formatInr` + `toDateTimeLocalInput`/`toDateInput` (`src/shared/lib/format`), and server-side `parseIdParam` + `pickDefined` (`src/shared/lib/api`). **Still open** (these need a designed component/hook API, not find-and-replace, so they carry regression risk — do as a scoped task, not a blind sweep):
   - **`useFormDialog()` / `<FormDialog>`** — the save→toast→loading→cancel flow duplicated across `TaskFormDialog`, `DealFormDialog`, `CreateLedgerDialog`, etc. (~400 lines).
   - **`usePortalPosition()` hook** — identical portal target + fixed-positioning `useLayoutEffect` logic in `InvProductCombobox` and `GroupCombobox` (~150 lines).
   - **Status/badge colour maps** — consolidate per-module `KIND_BADGE` / `TX_STATUS_COLORS` / `channelTone` into `src/shared/lib/constants/badges.ts` (~40 lines).
   - **Shared `<FormField>` (label+input grid), `RupeeInput` (₹-prefixed), `SaveButton` (spinner)** wrappers in `src/shared/components/`.
   - Lower-value, also deferred from the safe pass: the API **permission + try/catch + handleApiError** higher-order wrapper (~328 catch blocks) and shared **voucher cancel/delete** transaction orchestration (touches protected accounting math — coordinate with item 4).
9. **Make sub-pipeline spawning configurable, not hardcoded (raised 2026-06-11).** A "sub-pipeline" = a parent reaching a trigger status spawns a NEW child entity on its own pipeline (Order CONFIRMED → `spawnDelivery` on P8; release stage → `spawnRefund` on P9). Today the _spawn rule_ is a literal `if (statusId === ORDER_STAGE.CONFIRMED)` in [src/app/api/orders/[source]/[id]/status/route.ts](src/app/api/orders/[source]/[id]/status/route.ts) — it is the **only** "what happens next" primitive that is code instead of data. Every other one is configurable in the workflow settings UI: worker edges (`successSubStatus`/`failSubStatus` + `SubStatusTransition`), status transitions (`StatusTransition`), and `triggerMap` auto-advance. **Goal:** model spawn as a configurable transition outcome — a status's outgoing edge can be tagged "spawn `<pipelineId>` sub-pipeline" — so it joins the same data-driven edge model and the spawn hooks become a generic dispatcher reading config rows. **Stretch:** generalize the parent→child relation (currently order-only, polymorphic `orderSource`/`orderId`) so a repair **Ticket** can spawn the same Delivery sub-pipeline (e.g. ship device back); if so, **Escalation** and **Transfer Pipeline** likely collapse into this one "hand off / grow a sub-pipeline" mechanism instead of being bespoke buttons. Schema-change → same-commit `bootstrap-seed.ts` + dummy seed updates per the hard rule. Part of the broader Work-layer / workflow-engine redesign (see memory `workflow-access-work-layer-redesign`).
10. **Inbox: branch- / department-wise groups + status-aware system messages (raised 2026-07-28).** The inbox is **user-to-user messaging first**; groups are an optional layer. **Role groups were dropped** (2026-07-28) — job title is the wrong axis for a group chat. Two follow-ups:
   - **Branch / department groups replace them.** `ResolvedUser` already carries `branchIds` / `departmentIds` ([permissions.ts](src/shared/lib/auth/permissions.ts)), so this is a like-for-like swap of what role rooms did in [inbox.ts](src/modules/message/lib/inbox.ts): add `BRANCH_GROUP` / `DEPARTMENT_GROUP` to `ChatRoomType` plus `ChatRoom.branchId` / `.departmentId`, then mirror the old `roleId in user.roles` clause. Name rooms from real `Branch.name` / `Department.name` (role rooms used the raw id — ugly). Schema change → one `prisma db push`. **Also fix the early-return** at the top of `ensureCoreRoomsForUser`: it bails when the system room exists, so a user transferred to a new branch would never get that branch's room — harmless for roles, wrong for branches, where transfers are normal.
   - **~~System messages must follow the user's CURRENT status~~ — ✅ done 2026-09-04.** Recipients are resolved live from Position appointments (OR'd with the legacy `role` match) by the shared `findUserIdsHoldingRoles` helper; see the entry in **Recently shipped**. The deeper change it named — target by **permission**, not role id, since every caller passes `roleIds` — is still open.
11. **Timeline improvements — surface collected outcome values (raised 2026-06-15).** When a helper finishes a work on the Work Inbox, the **named outcome values** they type (e.g. _Relative's phone_, _Quoted price_) are merged into `Ticket.context` only — a flat, overwritable bag — and **never reach the ticket-page Timeline**. Today the timeline shows only the _result_ (Success/Unsuccess) and the _free-text note_; the structured answers are invisible on the ticket page and resurface only as _"From an earlier step"_ context in a later work's inbox card. **Goal:** write the collected args onto the `Work done` `TicketLog` row using the **dormant `TicketLog.data` (Json) column** at completion ([worker/route.ts](src/app/api/tickets/[id]/worker/route.ts)), then render them under that timeline row ([tickets/[id]/page.tsx](<src/app/(protected)/office/repairing/tickets/[id]/page.tsx>)) — a permanent **per-event** record instead of one overwritable `Ticket.context` slot. `TicketLog.data`/`outcome` columns already exist (kept dormant for exactly this). No schema change needed. Vocabulary: these are **outcome** values collected FROM the helper (see memory `workinbox-context-vs-outcome`). Broader pass = other timeline polish.
12. **Reconciliation flag — inventory version + date colouring (raised 2026-07-29).** The ledger half shipped 2026-07-29 (see Recently shipped): `Account.reconciledThrough` / `reconciledAt` / `reconciledById`, with an automatic **retreat** when a backdated voucher lands inside a reconciled window. Two follow-ups deliberately parked:

- **Inventory reconciliation** — the same watermark for stock. Open question first: what is the reconciled _unit_? Per product, per branch, or per product-branch pair (physical stock is counted per location, so per-branch is the likely answer). Then the same retreat rule on `InvStockLedger` writes. Reuse `retreatReconciliation()`'s shape; do NOT copy the ledger version onto a second table without settling the unit.
- **Colour the dates in the ledger table** — rows dated on/before `reconciledThrough` render as settled (muted/ticked), rows after it as still-open, so the watermark is visible on the transaction rows themselves and not only in the header badge. Purely presentational; the data is already on the detail payload.

---

7. **POS part-credit at checkout (asked 2026-09-11, plan agreed in principle, NOT built).** Lift the two guards only — `sale/route.ts` "less than invoice total" rejection and `CheckoutDialog` `onCredit > 0.01` block — keep `checkCreditLimit` as the sole gate, require an identified customer for any credit portion, walk-in stays blocked. Follow-ups from the same Vyapar comparison: (a) discount/write-off line on a receipt so a ₹5 short payment can close the bill; (b) POS return in CREDIT mode should `createLink` against the original sale's open balance; (c) put a separator between counter and number in `voucherRef.ts`. See **🧭 Reference: Vyapar backup comparison**.

## 🧭 Design: HR & People (agreed 2026-09-18, restrictions dropped 2026-09-20)

**Who is staff.** Staff = `User` + HR rows, 1:1. No Employee entity: POS shifts, tickets, sales and seats are all User-keyed, and a second identity would need mapping back at every one of them. A helper who never logs in still gets a User row (company-domain email, password held by admin). Deleting a User deletes the HR history with it — the admin confirm says so; **Mark as left** is the normal way out.

**Tables.** `StaffProfile` (`userId @id`; staff code, `joinedOn`, `leftOn` + reason, `employmentType` as a plain string, `weeklyOff Int[]`, personal / KYC, `bankJson` in the same six-field bag as `Person.bankJson`, `ledgerAccountId @unique`, `expenseAccountId`) and `StaffSalary` (revision rows; `linesJson` = `[{ name, kind: EARNING|DEDUCTION, amountPaise }]`, `grossPaise` / `netPaise` denormalised; the structure in force on a date is the latest row with `effectiveFrom <= date`, so a later payroll run can compute any past month). Money in HR rows is **paise `Int`** (exact in JSON); the GL is rupees and the conversion happens once, when payroll posts.

**Books.** Accrual, one ledger per person under **Salary Payable** (LIABILITY under Current Liabilities; `ACCOUNT_GROUPS.SALARY_PAYABLE`, seeded by `bootstrap-seed.ts`, find-or-created by `staffLedger.ts` for a database that only ever took the migration). Opened with the profile on its first save. A later month-end payroll posts ONE journal — Dr `expenseAccountId ?? "Salary & Wages"` / Cr each person's ledger; advances and salary payments are ordinary Payment vouchers against that same ledger (the Money tab links straight to `/vouchers/payments/new?partyAccountId=<ledger>`), so the balance always reads what is owed or what advance is outstanding. The four existing per-person expense ledgers (`Naba Salary` …) stay: each profile picks its own expense ledger.

**Restrictions: none.** No office-only sync rule (both tables replicate to every till like everything else), no read gate on the HR APIs (reads are default-open, as everywhere), the personal snapshot captures profiles and salaries in full. Owner, 2026-09-20: *"put no extra restriction … we will secure the app itself sooner."*

**Personal details live on the person's own page — the Employee Portal.** Owner: *"staff already has their profile, where they update own information, password etc."* — so the HR profile started as Job / Pay / Money (it is now Job / Access / Personal / Pay / Money / ID card — Access carries the role, manager and the login card, shared with Admin → Users). Since 2026-09-26 the person's own page is the **Employee Portal** at `/office/me` (`EmployeePortal`, groups Overview / Work / Leave / Pay / Me / Social; `/office/hr/me` redirects there on the same sidebar tick), where **Me → Personal & Bank** saves through the same HR PATCH under the same self-or-admin rule as `PUT /api/users/[id]`. A colleague's profile is `/office/user/[id]`; both are free routes.

**Leaving.** `leftOn` (a DATE, "no sign-in from this date") on the replicated table, so the counter refuses with the line down. The PATCH that sets it to today or earlier vacates the `UserPosition` rows (a left person must not keep resolving work) and bumps `tokensValidFrom`; a future date only blocks sign-in from that day. `findStaffUser` includes `staffProfile.leftOn`; both authorizers answer the same "Invalid email or password".

**Later phases — all built (see the dated entries above).** Attendance: `AttendanceDay` (`@@unique([userId, date])`, written by the till's first sign-in and the office register, graded on worked minutes), `Holiday` (org-wide or per branch), `LeaveRequest` + `LeaveType` quotas. Payroll: `PayrollRun` + `PayrollRunLine` posting one JOURNAL through `engineCreateVoucher`, paid in bulk through `postSettlementVoucher(kind: "PAYMENT")` from the Pay tab; the printable slip is the `PAYSLIP` kind in the print-template registry (`/receipt/payslip/[lineId]`, `PayslipBody`), per AGENTS.md §2.

## 🧭 Design: Universal Customer Finder (planned — 2026-06-05)

**Goal.** On a Front Office search screen, identify _which_ customer staff mean from **any** shred of evidence — name, phone, email, address, a product they **bought / repaired / inquired about / ordered online**, or **when** they were active (a month / "last active"). Each clue narrows the list; every match shows _why_ (evidence chip).

**Prototype state (was on `/lab/front`; deleted with `/lab` on 2026-09-27 — the code is in git at `cfe711ff`).**

- Identity: name, every phone, every email, address, ledger aliases and linked company names — one blob, matched in memory over the whole party list (`usePartySearch` → `partySearch.partySearchText`). _Was `/api/person?lookup=` with an opt-in `wide=1` for email + address (2026-06-05); `wide` became a no-op on 2026-09-11 and both it and the endpoint were removed on 2026-09-16._
- Repairs: **device brand/model/type + problem text** — `/api/tickets` is loaded **once into the browser** and filtered client-side.
- Scoring lives in `src/modules/lab/lib/buildCandidates.ts` (pure, framework-free): merges people-hits + tickets by `accountId`, scores by _how many distinct query terms have evidence_, emits typed `EvidenceTag`s rendered by `CandidateRow.tsx`.

**Architecture decision.** The "load all rows into the browser and filter" trick works for 2 sources but will **not** scale to sales + leads + shop orders + date filtering. Move to **one server-side finder endpoint**; `buildCandidates` was written pure specifically so its merge/score logic can migrate server-side unchanged.

```
GET /api/customers/find?q=rosy+laptop&month=2026-05&group=Sundry+Debtors
  → parallel server-side searches, each capped + term-filtered:
      identity   name/phone/email/address        (Person, Company, Phone, Email)
      repairs    device brand/model/problem       (Ticket → Device.accountId)
      sales      product bought                    (InvVoucherLine → InvVoucher.accountId, type SALE/POS)
      inquiries  interest/title/notes              (CrmLead → personId/companyId)
      shop       online order items                (ShopOrderLine → ShopOrder.accountId|personId)
  → merge by Account.id, score by terms-covered, optional date-window filter
  → ranked candidates + typed evidence ("Bought: Asus laptop · May 2026")
```

**Signal inventory** (link path → customer · when):
| Signal | Model (file) | Link to customer | When |
|---|---|---|---|
| Identity | `Person`/`Company`/`Phone`/`Email` (base.prisma) | self / `personId`/`companyId` → `Account` | `createdAt` |
| Repairs | `Ticket`/`Device` (tickets.prisma) | `Device.accountId` | `Ticket.createdAt`, `receivedBackAt` |
| Sales/POS/Estimate/Return | `InvVoucher`/`InvVoucherLine` (inventory.prisma) | `InvVoucher.accountId` | `InvVoucher.date` _(DB `date`, not DateTime)_ |
| Inquiries | `CrmLead` (crm.prisma) | `personId`/`companyId` | `createdAt`, `updatedAt` |
| Online orders | `ShopOrder`/`ShopOrderLine` (shop.prisma) | `accountId` _or legacy_ `personId` | `createdAt`, `paidAt`, `deliveredAt` |
| Payments/receipts | `Voucher` (finance.prisma) | `partyAccountId` | `date` — low ID value; narration only |
| Loyalty / coupons / gift cards | loyalty.prisma, coupons.prisma | `accountId` / `ownerAccountId` | various — low ID value; skip for finder |

**Phased roadmap.**

- **A — DONE (2026-06-05):** email + address matching (opt-in `wide=1`), new `email`/`address` evidence tags + chips.
- **B — Products bought:** sales-history matching. New `{ kind: "purchase" }` evidence tag (product name · date). Smallest standalone step; can be a `/api/customers/find` v0 scoped to sales only, OR a temporary `/api/inventory/products/sold-by` lookup.
- **C — Inquiries + online orders:** fold `CrmLead` (interest/title/notes) and `ShopOrderLine` (online purchases) into the finder. New `{ kind: "inquiry" }` / `{ kind: "order" }` tags.
- **D — Time / "when":** month + last-active dimension. UX: a dedicated **month/date chip** (not a free-text token — "may" is ambiguous as a word) that filters every signal's date window; also sort candidates by _latest activity_. Surface "Last active: May 2026" on each row.
- **E — Scale:** if volume warrants, a denormalized `CustomerActivityLog(accountId, signalType, signalId, date, summaryText, productSku)` (materialized view or sync job) so the finder is one indexed query instead of N parallel ones. Harmonize `InvVoucher.date` → indexed `DateTime` for range queries.

**Gotchas to honor when building.**

- Keep POS/party-picker search **strict** — only the finder opts into wide/multi-signal.
- `InvVoucher.date` is a DB `date`, not `DateTime` — month-range filtering must account for that.
- Never surface raw `GL-…` refs; resolve purchase evidence to the originating voucher number.
- Branch scoping is not yet enforced on ticket/sale/customer queries (see item 6) — the finder should be ready to filter by `branchId` once that lands.
- Sources to reference when implementing: `buildCandidates.ts`, `CandidateRow.tsx`, `usePartySearch.ts`, `/api/person/route.ts` (the `wide` pattern), `/api/inventory/vouchers`, `/api/tickets`, `/api/crm/leads`, `/api/shop/orders`.

3. **POS follow-ups**: return receipt print page (`/receipt/return/[id]`); multi-partial-return guard (subtract prior returns from per-line cap); stuck-shift auto-close cron; per-row bank account picker for UPI/Card.
4. **Outstandings — Pay Now / Receive Now action** — the linking half landed 2026-09-08: Ledger-Outstandings and the Receivables bill rows each carry a per-row link button that settles the bill against the party's opposite-side vouchers. What remains is the *shortcut* — a button that opens a **pre-filled** Receipt/Payment voucher for a bill with no matching voucher yet, so the user does not copy the ref and amount into a blank one. Payables still has no bill-wise tab (party level only), and a party who is both customer and supplier holds ONE ledger under ONE group, so their other side shows on Ledger-Outstandings rather than in the opposite summary report — giving such a contact two ledgers is a separate, larger job the owner has ruled out for now.
5. **Repair-side reporting** — tickets by status/pipeline, avg resolution, technician workload. Finance side is covered; repair side is bare.
6. **Branch-scoped data filtering** — permissions exist but ticket/customer/invoice queries don't filter by `branchId`.
7. **Ticket list pagination** — current list loads all rows.
8. **Accountancy Phase 5b** — GSTR-1/3B export, e-Invoice (IRN) integration, RCM on purchases.
9. **Portal ticket detail** — full notes + status history, not just the list.
10. **Stock decrement on shop order confirm** — now possible since `InvProduct` is unified and multi-branch stock is live.
11. **Razorpay payment status page** — post-payment redirect with real-time polling.
12. **`/api/finance/*` → `/api/accountancy/*`** rename (still deferred from the 2026-05-07 module rename).
13. **Credit-limit enforcement on an EDIT of a posted sale** — the create and post paths are covered (2026-09-16, both office callers via `postVoucherInTx`, plus POS). `PATCH /api/inventory/vouchers/[id]` is not: the customer's balance already carries that bill's old AR, so the honest test is `balance − oldOnCredit + newOnCredit`, and the plain `balance + new` used everywhere else would wrongly refuse ordinary edits. Needs the old settlement read before the rewrite. **Set real credit limits first** — most customers have none, and auto is capped at ₹1,000.
14. **Schema migration hygiene** — GST/HSN + naming-convention columns were added via `db push`, no migration. Needs a proper migration before any prod deploy.
15. **Franchisee support** — model independent shops that use your brand but are separate legal entities (own PAN, own GSTIN). Distinct from company-owned `Branch` (same legal entity, your stock, your cash, your books) on every dimension that matters: a franchisee is a `Company` row under Sundry Debtors, sales to them are real B2B vouchers (IGST / CGST+SGST per state), their end-customer sales never enter your books, and royalty is a separate ledger. Smallest first step: add `isFranchisee Boolean @default(false)` + `royaltyPercent Decimal?` + `franchiseStartedAt DateTime?` on `Company`; surface a "Franchisee" filter / badge in customer lists; partner-portal access (one login per franchisee proprietor) reusing existing customer-portal infra. Bigger follow-ups: monthly royalty invoice generation, stock advisory dashboards, contract documents. See chat 2026-05-23 for the full company-owned-vs-franchisee comparison.
16. **Loyalty points** — earn/redeem points on POS + shop sales; per-customer balance, configurable accrual rate, redemption as discount or store credit; ledger-backed so liability shows on the books.
17. **Inactive masters excluded from all dropdowns** — Warranty Terms, Units, Categories, Groups, Brands, etc. marked `isActive = false` should not appear in any dropdown/select UI across the app. Affects: warranty-term picker on products, unit select on voucher lines, brand/category filters, etc. Start with warranty terms (seen on inventory/masters/warranty-terms).
18. **Long-term**: SLA alerts; B2B Company portal; ERP module foundation; per-customer pricing tiers.
